SECTION 0 — Repositories / project locations  [BACKFILL — verify]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Backend: hub-finproms-backend; Frontend: hub-finproms-frontend (separate repos).
  - Whenever implementing or changing a feature, apply related changes in the
    frontend as well (UI, API calls, roles, branding, permissions, etc.).
  - Do not treat backend-only work as complete if the feature also requires
    frontend updates.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0-A  Dual-repo completeness (process / acceptance)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-0-001 | [PROCESS] Feature with UI impact has matching frontend work
Precondition: Ticket/PR changes an API consumed by the SPA
Steps: Review backend PR + frontend PR/branch
Expected: Frontend updated (calls, types, roles, branding, permissions) or
          ticket explicitly documents FE follow-up before “done”

TC-0-002 | [PROCESS] Backend-only change is labelled as such
Precondition: Change is pure server (migration, job, no UI)
Steps: Check acceptance notes
Expected: Marked backend-only; no false claim of full feature complete

TC-0-003 | [PROCESS] Capability / role / branding API changes update FE
Precondition: Matrix key, role label, or branding field added/changed
Steps: Inspect frontend capabilities/nav/branding consumers
Expected: FE reads new keys; old hard-codes removed where required

TC-0-004 | [PROCESS] Definition of Done checklist includes both repos
Steps: Close a cross-cutting feature
Expected: DoD requires FE+BE verification when UI is in scope

TC-0-005 | [PROCESS] Repo locations documented for the team
Steps: Confirm PROJECT_REQUIREMENTS §0 paths vs actual clones
Expected: Backend and frontend paths match team workspace convention

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0 TRACEABILITY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Requirement bullet                              | Test IDs
------------------------------------------------|------------------------------
Separate backend / frontend repos               | 005
Apply FE changes with BE features               | 001–004
Do not treat BE-only as complete when UI needed | 001–002

================================================================================
SECTION 1 — Product overview (hub types + branding)  [BACKFILL — verify]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - FinProms content platform: posts, reels, etc.
  - Users subscribe, receive credits, spend on posts/reels, then download/preview.
  - Branding (colours, logo, etc.) from DATABASE — each hub looks different
    without code changes.
  - THREE hub types, ONE codebase, separate DBs/.env per instance:
      A) Central Hub Controller — control plane only (not member catalog)
      B) Shared Hub — public/shared content catalog; remotely controlled
      C) White-labelled Hub — private client; branding from DB; remotely controlled
  - Central is master registry / remote-control source — not Shared Hub.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1-A  Hub type identity & isolation
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1-001 | [BE] Instance reports type via GET /api/hub (or /api/version)
Steps: Call on Central, Shared, WL deploys
Expected: type / is_control_plane correct per instance (central vs shared vs
          white_label)

TC-1-002 | [BE] Central is_control_plane true; content hubs false
Steps: Compare /api/version on each
Expected: Only Central is_control_plane=true

TC-1-003 | [BE] Each instance uses its own database (no shared content DB)
Steps: Create a post on Shared A; query Central and WL B
Expected: Post exists only on Shared A DB

TC-1-004 | [BE] HUB_SLUG / .env identify the instance
Steps: Inspect config hub.current_slug on each deploy
Expected: Distinct slug; FRONTEND_URL / APP_URL / DB_* per instance

TC-1-005 | [BE] Same codebase artefact can boot as any hub type
Steps: Deploy same release with different .env/type
Expected: Behaviour follows type (control plane vs content hub)

TC-1-006 | [BE] Central is NOT a substitute member catalog
Steps: As ordinary Central user attempt Shared-style browse/buy
Expected: Member catalog off by default; no replacement of Shared catalog

TC-1-007 | [BE] Shared is NOT the control plane for other hubs
Steps: On Shared, attempt Central-only hub registry / remote control of peers
Expected: Not available (control lives on Central)

TC-1-008 | [BE] White-label is private / restricted vs Shared public patterns
Steps: Compare public_subscribe / one_off cash / advisor flags defaults
Expected: WL defaults match private client model (credits-only cash rules etc.)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1-B  Branding from database
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1-009 | [BE] GET /api/hub exposes branding from DB
Steps: GET /api/hub
Expected: branding/application_name, logo_url, colours (or equivalent) from hub
          settings/DB — not only hard-coded env theme

TC-1-010 | [BE] Changing logo/colours in DB/settings changes public hub payload
Steps: Update hub logo_url / colour settings; GET /api/hub again
Expected: New values returned without code redeploy

TC-1-011 | [FE] Two hubs with different DB branding look different
Steps: Open Shared vs WL frontends
Expected: Distinct logos/colours/app name from each hub DB

TC-1-012 | [BE] Branding not overwritten by deploy package apply
Steps: Apply code update package; re-check branding fields
Expected: .env/storage preserved; branding rows unchanged

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1-C  Core product loop (smoke — detail in §2.4/§2.5)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1-013 | [FE+BE] Smoke: subscribe → credits → buy post/reel → preview
Steps: On Shared hub complete minimal path
Expected: Works end-to-end (detailed cases in §2.4/§2.5)

TC-1-014 | [BE] Content types include posts and reels at platform level
Steps: Seed/list types; create one of each
Expected: Platform supports both (detail §2.1/§2.2)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------|------------------------------
Three hub types + one codebase + own DB/.env    | 001–008
Central = control plane; Shared/WL = content    | 002, 006–007
Branding from database                          | 009–012
Subscribe / credits / buy / preview loop        | 013–014

SECTION 1A — Central Hub Controller (control plane)  [BACKFILL — verify]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Separate remote control from content hubs.
  - Power Admin on Central: (1) hub registry (2) deploy wiring (3) Functionalities
    + Modules (4) capabilities matrix (5) PA own checklist (6) platform payments
    (7) remote control / hub switcher (8) code updates.
  - Spawn: copy codebase → own DB/.env → register on Central with wiring.
  - Registry on Central DB; content catalogs on each hub DB.
  - Ready = active + frontend_url + remote DB credentials.
  - Badges: Wiring ready / Needs wiring; code version badges.
  - Central ≠ Shared catalog.

Actors:
  PA = Power Admin with pa_manage_hubs (and related pa_*)
  X  = Non–power-admin / missing pa caps

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1A-A  Hub registry
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1A-001 | [BE] PA can create Shared hub registry row
Steps: POST power-admin hubs { type: shared, name, slug, … }
Expected: 201; type=shared; appears in list

TC-1A-002 | [BE] PA can create White-label hub registry row
Steps: POST type=white_label
Expected: 201; type=white_label; defaults follow WL packaging

TC-1A-003 | [BE] PA can list all Shared + WL hubs
Steps: GET hub list
Expected: Both types returned; Central may be listed separately/local

TC-1A-004 | [BE] PA can edit hub registry fields
Steps: PUT name/urls/active flags
Expected: 200; persisted on Central DB only

TC-1A-005 | [BE] Without pa_manage_hubs → 403
Steps: As X
Expected: 403 on registry mutate/list as gated

TC-1A-006 | [BE] Duplicate slug rejected
Steps: Create second hub same slug
Expected: 422

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1A-B  Deploy wiring & ready badges
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1A-007 | [BE] Store frontend_url, api_url, db_* (password encrypted)
Steps: PUT wiring with db_password
Expected: 200; password encrypted at rest; plaintext not returned raw

TC-1A-008 | [BE] deploy_notes + copyable env snippet available
Steps: GET hub detail / wiring payload
Expected: Notes + env checklist/snippet for ops

TC-1A-009 | [BE] Ready = active + frontend_url + remote DB credentials
Steps: Hub missing one of three; then complete all
Expected: Needs wiring → Wiring ready badge flips when complete

TC-1A-010 | [FE] Hub list shows Wiring ready / Needs wiring
Steps: Open Central Hubs page
Expected: Badges match ready rule

TC-1A-011 | [BE] Incomplete wiring blocks remote DB actions
Steps: Attempt remote list content without DB creds
Expected: Fail with clear wiring error

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1A-C  Functionalities, Modules, Capabilities, PA checklist, payments
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1A-012 | [BE] PA can GET/PUT hub Functionalities checklist (§5.1)
Steps: Toggle a functionality for target hub
Expected: Saved on registry/hub config Central manages

TC-1A-013 | [BE] PA can GET/PUT Modules checklist per hub (§5.1A)
Steps: Enable Social Media Pre Approval on a hub
Expected: Module flag persisted; hub behaviour follows when connected

TC-1A-014 | [BE] PA can edit user capabilities matrix per hub (§5.2)
Steps: Toggle smc_review_requests for Approver on hub X
Expected: Saved; enforced when acting on / connected to that hub

TC-1A-015 | [BE] PA own capabilities checklist (§5.3) gates platform tools
Steps: Disable a pa_* for self/role; attempt tool
Expected: 403 when off; allowed when on

TC-1A-016 | [BE] Platform payment methods configurable on Central
Steps: Enable/disable Stripe/bank platform settings as designed
Expected: Settings persist; used as platform fallback where product says

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1A-D  Remote control / hub switcher
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1A-017 | [BE] PA can act on wired Shared hub DB (content taxonomy)
Precondition: Wiring ready + remote-control / receive flags allow
Steps: Switch acting hub; GET/POST types or posts on that hub
Expected: Reads/writes target hub DB; Central content DB unchanged

TC-1A-018 | [BE] PA can act on wired White-label hub similarly
Steps: Same as above for WL
Expected: Target WL DB only

TC-1A-019 | [BE] Acting hub blocked when flags/wiring disallow
Steps: Disable remote-control or strip DB creds
Expected: Acting writes rejected

TC-1A-020 | [FE] Hub switcher shows eligible hubs; ineligible disabled + reason
Steps: Open switcher with mixed ready/not-ready hubs
Expected: Ineligible visible but disabled with reason

TC-1A-021 | [BE] Content catalogs remain on hub DBs — Central does not store them
Steps: Create post while acting on Shared; inspect Central posts table
Expected: Post on Shared DB only

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1A-E  Spawn workflow (ops + registry)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1A-022 | [PROCESS] Spawn Shared: copy codebase → own DB/.env → register
Steps: Follow ops checklist; PA registers hub
Expected: Registry row type=shared; wiring fields fillable; instance boots alone

TC-1A-023 | [PROCESS] Spawn WL same pattern with type=white_label
Steps: Register WL
Expected: Defaults/modules follow white_label type

TC-1A-024 | [BE] New hub does not share Central content database
Steps: After spawn, create local content
Expected: Isolated DB

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1A-F  Central ≠ member catalog
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1A-025 | [BE] Member browse/buy not default on Central
Steps: GET catalog endpoints as ordinary Central user
Expected: Denied / catalog off unless member_* explicitly enabled

TC-1A-026 | [FE+BE] E2E: register hub → wire → refresh badges → act on hub
Steps: Full PA path
Expected: Ready badge; remote list/create taxonomy succeeds

SECTION 1B — Code updates across hubs (Phase 1 — tracking)  [BACKFILL — verify]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Track which hubs are on which release after manual Git/FTP deploy.
  - NOT new-hub spawn.
  - VERSION file and/or APP_VERSION / FRONTEND_VERSION.
  - GET /api/version → version, backend_version, frontend_version, slug, type,
    is_control_plane; also on GET /api/hub as hub.code.
  - Central: hub_releases (is_latest); per-hub code_version* + status
    (up_to_date|behind|ahead|unknown|error), source, checked_at, error.
  - PA APIs: list/publish releases; refresh-versions (all/one); mark-version.
  - Hub admin payloads include code_update summary.

Actors:
  PA = Power Admin pa_manage_hubs
  OPS = Deploy operator

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1B-A  Public version identity
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1B-001 | [BE] GET /api/version returns this deploy’s versions
Steps: GET /api/version on Central/Shared/WL
Expected: 200; version, backend_version, frontend_version, slug, type,
          is_control_plane

TC-1B-002 | [BE] GET /api/hub includes hub.code version info
Steps: GET /api/hub
Expected: code / version fields present matching deploy

TC-1B-003 | [BE] Bumping VERSION or APP_VERSION changes /api/version
Steps: Change version config; GET again
Expected: New version reported (no registry publish required for local report)

TC-1B-004 | [BE] FRONTEND_VERSION independently reportable
Steps: Set frontend_version ≠ backend
Expected: Both fields returned distinctly

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1B-B  Publish release & overview
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1B-005 | [BE] PA publishes release → is_latest row
Steps: POST /api/power-admin/releases { version, backend_version,
        frontend_version?, notes? }
Expected: 201; is_latest=true; previous latest cleared; overview shows latest

TC-1B-006 | [BE] GET /api/power-admin/releases lists releases + this_deploy
Steps: GET releases
Expected: latest_release + this_deploy versions

TC-1B-007 | [BE] Without pa_manage_hubs → 403 on publish/list
Steps: As non-PA
Expected: 403

TC-1B-008 | [BE] Duplicate version publish rejected or idempotent
Steps: Publish same version twice
Expected: Documented: 422 or safe no-dupe latest — _______________

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1B-C  Refresh / mark versions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1B-009 | [BE] Refresh Central uses local config (not HTTP loopback required)
Steps: POST .../hubs/{central}/refresh-version
Expected: code_version matches local; status vs latest computed

TC-1B-010 | [BE] Refresh content hub polls GET {api_url}/version
Precondition: Hub wired with reachable api_url
Steps: POST refresh one hub
Expected: Stores reported version; status up_to_date|behind|ahead|…;
          source=remote_api; checked_at set

TC-1B-011 | [BE] Refresh all hubs polls each eligible hub
Steps: POST .../hubs/refresh-versions
Expected: Each hub updated or error recorded per hub without aborting all

TC-1B-012 | [BE] Unreachable hub → status error + error message
Steps: Refresh hub with bad api_url
Expected: status=error; error text; no crash of batch

TC-1B-013 | [BE] mark-version manual when API unreachable
Steps: POST .../hubs/{hub}/mark-version { version }
Expected: source=manual; version stored; status recomputed vs latest

TC-1B-014 | [BE] Status labels: up_to_date / behind / ahead / unknown / error
Steps: Arrange versions relative to latest
Expected: Correct status_label for each case

TC-1B-015 | [FE] Hub list code version badges match statuses
Steps: Open Central Hubs page after refresh
Expected: Up to date / Behind latest / Unknown / Check failed (etc.)

TC-1B-016 | [BE] Hub admin payload includes code_update block
Steps: GET hub admin/detail as designed
Expected: reported_version, status, status_label, latest_version, …

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1B-D  Ops workflow smoke
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1B-017 | [PROCESS] Ship → bump versions → Publish → Refresh → badges
Steps: Ops workflow on two hubs
Expected: Badges reflect who is behind after publish

TC-1B-018 | [BE] Phase 1 does not apply zip packages (track only)
Steps: Publish without artifacts; refresh
Expected: Tracking works; no filesystem apply side effects

SECTION 1C — Code updates across hubs (Phase 2 — apply)  [BACKFILL — verify]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - From Central, apply release packages (backend zip and/or frontend dist zip)
    to selected existing hubs. Not spawn.
  - Artifacts: storage/app/private/releases/{ver}/; upload on publish or
    POST .../releases/{id}/artifacts (at least one zip to apply).
  - Backend zip: Laravel tree; never overwrite .env, storage/app|framework|logs,
    bootstrap/cache, .git.
  - Frontend zip → hub.code_frontend_path or CODE_UPDATE_FRONTEND_PATH.
  - POST /api/power-admin/releases/apply { release_id?, hub_ids[] }
  - Uses code_update_token (not backup/restore tokens); internal apply +
    artifact download APIs; migrate --force; clear caches; write VERSION.
  - Per hub apply_status idle|updating|applied|failed; history table
    hub_code_update_events; GET history.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1C-A  Artifacts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1C-001 | [BE] Upload backend_zip and/or frontend_zip on publish/artifacts
Steps: POST multipart artifacts for a release
Expected: Stored under private/releases/{ver}/; ready_to_apply when ≥1 zip

TC-1C-002 | [BE] Apply rejected when release has no artifacts
Steps: POST apply for release without zips
Expected: 422/error; hubs unchanged

TC-1C-003 | [BE] Artifact download requires code-update auth (not backup key)
Steps: GET internal artifacts with wrong/missing key; with code_update key
Expected: 401/403 wrong; 200 with valid code update auth

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1C-B  Apply to selected hubs
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1C-004 | [BE] Apply to selected hub_ids only
Precondition: Two hubs; artifacts ready
Steps: POST apply { hub_ids: [A] }
Expected: Only A updating/applied; B untouched

TC-1C-005 | [BE] Content hub apply via internal API with X-Hub-Code-Update-Key
Steps: Apply to wired Shared/WL
Expected: Hub downloads artifacts, extracts, writes VERSION, migrate --force,
          caches cleared; apply_status=applied; version refresh

TC-1C-006 | [BE] Central self-apply uses local files (no HTTP loopback)
Steps: Include Central in hub_ids
Expected: Applies from local disk; success without calling own public URL

TC-1C-007 | [BE] Backend extract never overwrites .env / storage / bootstrap/cache / .git
Steps: Apply backend zip that also contains dummy .env
Expected: Existing .env preserved; forbidden paths untouched

TC-1C-008 | [BE] Frontend extract lands in code_frontend_path
Precondition: code_frontend_path set on registry
Steps: Apply frontend_zip
Expected: Dist contents extracted to configured path

TC-1C-009 | [BE] Failed apply sets apply_status=failed + apply_error
Steps: Force failure (bad zip / unreachable)
Expected: failed status; error message; history row failed

TC-1C-010 | [BE] Successful apply then refresh shows up_to_date vs latest
Steps: Apply latest; refresh versions
Expected: reported version matches; status up_to_date

TC-1C-011 | [BE] code_update_token pushed/synced; not reused backup tokens
Steps: Inspect token fields / auth headers used
Expected: Distinct code_update_token / HUB_CODE_UPDATE_SECRET path only

TC-1C-012 | [BE] Without pa_manage_hubs → 403 on apply
Steps: As non-PA
Expected: 403

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1C-C  History & UI
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-1C-013 | [BE] hub_code_update_events written on Apply and mark-version
Steps: Apply success/fail; mark manual
Expected: Append-only events with hub, release, version, action, status,
          flags, triggered_by, finished_at

TC-1C-014 | [BE] GET /api/power-admin/releases/history?hub_id=&limit=
Steps: Filter by hub
Expected: Only that hub’s events; limit respected

TC-1C-015 | [BE] GET releases overview includes history
Steps: GET /api/power-admin/releases
Expected: overview.history present

TC-1C-016 | [FE] Central Hubs: upload zips, multi-select, Apply, history table
Steps: Use UI
Expected: Can publish+upload, select hubs, apply latest or prior ready release,
          see Update history (filter by hub); hub detail shows code_frontend_path

TC-1C-017 | [BE] Apply prior ready_to_apply release (package rollback)
Steps: Apply older release that still has artifacts
Expected: Hub moves to that package version; distinct from module DR §6B.1

TC-1C-018 | [FE+BE] E2E: publish with zips → apply to Shared → version badge updates
Steps: Full path
Expected: Applied hub reports new version; Behind clears

================================================================================
SECTION 2.1 — Content classification (THREE separate fields)
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - TYPE — what the item is (post, reel, …); managed list; drives reel play button.
  - CATEGORY — topical grouping; separate from type; optional Font Awesome icon
    and/or uploaded image (icon_path → icon_url via /api/media). Upload wins over
    font icon. Browse-by-Categories render order: upload → font → name initial.
    Central category CRUD syncs icons/name to eligible content hubs on distribute.
  - TAGS — free labels for filtering; separate from type and category.
  - Every content item has type + category + tags.
  - Do NOT treat category as type.

Capability keys (from matrix / routes):
  List:  member_browse_catalog OR dashboard_central_content_library OR
         dashboard_manage_types|categories|tags OR
         dashboard_view_types|categories|tags
  Mutate types:      dashboard_manage_types
  Mutate categories: dashboard_manage_categories
  Mutate tags:       dashboard_manage_tags
  Note: Manage types/categories/tags is Central-library oriented; DISABLED by
        default on Shared / White-label (view-only). Acting-hub switcher can
        still write to the selected hub’s own DB.

APIs:
  GET/POST/PUT/DELETE  /api/types
  GET/POST/PUT|POST/DELETE  /api/categories
  GET/POST/PUT/DELETE  /api/tags
  POST/PUT  /api/posts   (classification fields)
  (Acting hub) same paths and /api/hub-content/types|categories|tags

Actors:
  A0 = No token (guest)
  A1 = Authenticated member WITH member_browse_catalog
  A2 = Authenticated user WITHOUT catalog/view/manage taxonomy caps
  A3 = User WITH dashboard_view_types / view_categories / view_tags (list-only)
  A4 = User WITH dashboard_manage_types (and/or categories/tags as stated)
  A5 = Power Admin on Central (library + hub switcher)
  A6 = Hub-admin on Shared / White-label (manage taxonomy typically OFF)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

2.1-E  Negative / edge / security
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.1-093 | SQL / script injection in type name stored safely
Steps: POST type name with <script> or ' OR 1=1 --
Expected: Stored as plain text or sanitized; no XSS/SQL break; 201 or 422

TC-2.1-094 | Extremely long unicode / emoji names
Steps: Create type/category/tag with unicode within max length
Expected: Accepted if ≤100; returned correctly in UTF-8

TC-2.1-095 | Concurrent duplicate create
Steps: Two parallel POSTs same unique name
Expected: One 201; other 422 (no duplicate rows)

TC-2.1-096 | Category icon upload — disallowed file type rejected
Steps: Upload .exe / .php / oversized file as icon_file
Expected: 422; no path stored

TC-2.1-097 | Category icon_url not guessable private path
Steps: Create category with upload; inspect icon_url
Expected: Served via /api/media/... public disk path only

TC-2.1-098 | Cross-capability isolation
Precondition: User has manage_types but NOT manage_categories/tags
Steps: POST type OK; POST category/tag forbidden
Expected: 201 for type; 403 for category and tag

TC-2.1-099 | View capability cannot mutate
Precondition: Only dashboard_view_categories
Steps: POST/PUT/DELETE category
Expected: List 200; mutations 403

TC-2.1-100 | Idempotent read — repeated GET list unchanged
Steps: GET types/categories/tags twice
Expected: Same data; no side effects

================================================================================
SECTION 2.2 — Content browsing & listing UX          [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Hub catalog / main member pages require login. Guests are sent to login and
    must not see the hub main/catalog UI.
  - Users browse posts, reels, and other types.
  - Listing page: 3-column grid (2 tablet, 1 mobile).  [FRONTEND]
  - REELS: admin can upload a video when type is Reel.
  - Reel video autoplays muted + looping on listing and detail.  [FRONTEND]
  - Play/pause “Reel” control at top of video.  [FRONTEND]
  - Reels without video still show legacy centered play indicator on listing. [FE]
  - "NEW" banner when item is within the "new" window; duration from DATABASE
    setting `new_banner_days` (not hard-coded). Default 7; API exposes
    `is_new` and `new_banner_days`.

APIs / settings in scope:
  GET  /api/posts?type=&category=&tag=&search=&per_page=
  GET  /api/posts/{id}
  POST /api/posts  (reel video attachment) — capability-gated
  PUT  /api/posts/{id}
  GET/PUT settings including new_banner_days (dashboard_manage_settings)
  Response fields: is_reel, is_new, is_video, video_url, cover_url,
                   can_view_catalog, new_banner_days, content_type

Actors:
  G  = Guest (no token)
  M  = Authenticated member with member_browse_catalog
  N  = Authenticated user without catalog capability
  CA = Hub-admin / role that can manage settings
  CL = Central library / manage_posts role (create reel + video)

Layer tags:
  [BE] = backend API / data
  [FE] = frontend UX (verify in hub-finproms-frontend)
  [FE+BE] = both

================================================================================
SECTION 2.2a — Shared Hub self-registration email verification  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - When public self-registration is enabled (`public_subscribe` / not invite-only),
    new members must verify email before they can sign in.
  - Register: account with email_verified_at=null; NO Sanctum token; sends
    hub-branded link `{frontend}/verify-email?token=…&email=…` (expires 60 min).
  - Verify: marks verified, sends welcome email, returns auth token (signed in).
  - Login blocked for unverified with `email_verification_required`; resend
    throttled (60 seconds).
  - Admin-created users marked verified immediately.
  - Excel-imported users marked verified immediately (Import Users flow itself
    is SKIPPED in this master file — only the verified-outcome assertion here).
  - Existing accounts backfilled verified by migration (not locked out).
  - Email template catalog event: `email_verification`.
  - APIs: POST /api/auth/register, /api/auth/verify-email,
          /api/auth/resend-verification, /api/auth/login
  - Frontend: Register “check your email”; VerifyEmail page; Login resend offer.

Constants (implementation):
  EmailVerificationService::EXPIRE_MINUTES = 60
  EmailVerificationService::THROTTLE_SECONDS = 60

Actors:
  G  = Guest
  U  = Unverified self-registered member
  V  = Verified member
  AD = Admin / hub-admin who can create users
  PA = Power Admin / template editor

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.2a-A  Registration gate (public_subscribe)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Steps: POST /api/auth/register { name, email, password, password_confirmation }
Expected: 201; user row created

TC-2.2a-002 | [BE] Register rejected when public_subscribe OFF / invite-only
Precondition: public_subscribe=false
Steps: POST /api/auth/register with valid payload

          Record status: _______________

Expected: 422 for each; no user / no token

Steps: POST register same email
Expected: 422 unique; no second account

TC-2.2a-005 | [FE] Register form hidden or blocked when invite-only
Precondition: public_subscribe OFF

Expected: Cannot complete public self-registration (redirect/message)

================================================================================
SECTION 2.2b — Two-factor authentication (login OTP) + Update profile  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - NOT a hub Functionalities checkbox. Per-user opt-in on Update profile
    (`users.two_factor_enabled`, default OFF).
  - Only that user gets email OTP after successful password check on every login.
  - Enable/disable 2FA (and changing email) requires `current_password`.
  - Login returns otp_required + otp_challenge (no Sanctum token) when OTP on
    and MFA not enrolled. 6-digit code emailed; expires 10 min; max 5 fails;
    resend throttled 60s.
  - APIs: POST /api/auth/verify-login-otp, POST /api/auth/resend-login-otp,
          PUT/PATCH/POST /api/auth/profile, POST /api/auth/login, GET /api/hub
  - GET /hub: auth.login_otp_per_user=true; auth.login_otp_required=false (legacy)
  - Email template event: login_otp
  - FE Login second step when otp_required
  - Activity: auth.two_factor_otp_chosen / auth.two_factor_otp_disabled
    (properties.method = otp)
  - Profile (all authenticated users): name, email, avatar, email OTP toggle,
    MFA toggle (MFA detail in §2.2b2). Email change clears verification +
    sends new link. Avatar on public disk avatars/{user_id}/… via /api/media/

Constants:
  LoginOtpService::EXPIRE_MINUTES = 10
  LoginOtpService::THROTTLE_SECONDS = 60
  LoginOtpService::MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5

Actors:
  G  = Guest
  U0 = Verified user, two_factor_enabled=false, no MFA
  U1 = Verified user, two_factor_enabled=true, no MFA
  UM = User with MFA enrolled (mfa takes precedence — light cross-check)
  A  = Authenticated user updating own profile

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.2b-A  Not hub-wide — per-user default OFF
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.2b-001 | [BE] GET /api/hub exposes login_otp_per_user=true
Steps: GET /api/hub as G
Expected: hub.auth.login_otp_per_user = true

TC-2.2b-002 | [BE] GET /api/hub login_otp_required always false (legacy)
Steps: GET /api/hub
Expected: hub.auth.login_otp_required = false
          (even if some users have OTP on)

TC-2.2b-003 | [BE] New user defaults two_factor_enabled OFF
Steps: Create/register user; inspect DB / profile payload
Expected: two_factor_enabled=false

TC-2.2b-004 | [BE] No hub Functionalities checkbox required for OTP
Steps: Hub checklist / functionalities UI
Expected: No require_login_otp (or equivalent) hub-wide flag controlling login OTP

TC-2.2b-005 | [BE] OTP applies on Shared, White-label, and Central (all hub types)
Precondition: U1 on each hub type under test
Steps: Login with OTP enabled
Expected: otp_required flow on each hub type tested

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.2b-B  Enable / disable OTP on Update profile
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.2b-006 | [BE] Enable OTP with correct current_password
Precondition: A = U0 authenticated
Steps: PUT/PATCH /api/auth/profile
       { two_factor_enabled: true, current_password: "<correct>" }
Expected: 200; two_factor_enabled=true

================================================================================
SECTION 2.2b2 — Authenticator MFA (Microsoft Authenticator / TOTP)  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Per-user checkbox on Update profile (`users.mfa_enabled`, default OFF).
    Compatible with Microsoft Authenticator and any RFC 6238 TOTP app.
  - Mutually exclusive with email OTP: enabling one clears the other.
  - Enable is two-step:
      (1) profile mfa_enabled=true + current_password → encrypted secret stored;
          returns mfa_setup { secret, otpauth_uri, issuer, account } with
          mfa_confirmation_required; user is mfa_pending until confirmed.
      (2) POST /api/auth/mfa/confirm with app code + password →
          mfa_enabled + mfa_confirmed_at.
  - Optional: POST /api/auth/mfa/setup to resume/regenerate pending setup.
  - Disable: profile mfa_enabled=false + password clears secret.
  - Login when enrolled: mfa_required + mfa_challenge (no token).
    POST /api/auth/verify-login-mfa { email, challenge, code }.
    MFA takes precedence over email OTP if both somehow set.
  - GET /hub: auth.login_mfa_per_user = true.
  - Activity: auth.mfa_setup_started, auth.mfa_chosen, auth.mfa_disabled,
    auth.login_mfa_required, auth.login_mfa_verified (properties.method = mfa).

Constants:
  LoginMfaChallengeService::EXPIRE_MINUTES = 10
  LoginMfaChallengeService::MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5

Actors:
  G  = Guest
  U0 = Verified user, MFA off, OTP off
  UP = User with MFA pending (secret set, not confirmed)
  UE = User with MFA enrolled (mfa_enabled + mfa_confirmed_at + secret)
  UO = User with email OTP on only
  A  = Authenticated self on Update profile

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.2b2-A  Defaults, hub flags, mutual exclusivity
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.2b2-001 | [BE] GET /api/hub exposes login_mfa_per_user=true
Steps: GET /api/hub
Expected: hub.auth.login_mfa_per_user = true

TC-2.2b2-002 | [BE] New user defaults mfa_enabled OFF / not pending
Steps: Create user; inspect profile/me
Expected: mfa_enabled=false; mfa_pending=false; mfa_confirmed_at null

TC-2.2b2-003 | [BE] Cannot enable OTP and MFA in same profile request
Steps: PUT profile { two_factor_enabled:true, mfa_enabled:true, current_password }
Expected: 422; validation error on mfa_enabled (or equivalent)

TC-2.2b2-004 | [BE] Enabling MFA clears email OTP
Precondition: UO has two_factor_enabled=true
Steps: Start MFA setup (mfa_enabled=true + password) then confirm
Expected: After confirm: mfa enrolled; two_factor_enabled=false

TC-2.2b2-005 | [BE] Choosing email OTP clears enrolled MFA + secret
Precondition: UE enrolled
Steps: PUT profile { two_factor_enabled:true, current_password }
Expected: OTP on; mfa_enabled=false; mfa_secret null; mfa_confirmed_at null;
          activity auth.two_factor_otp_chosen and auth.mfa_disabled

TC-2.2b2-006 | [BE] MFA available on all hub types (Shared / WL / Central)
Steps: Enroll + login MFA on each hub type under test
Expected: Same per-user behaviour on each

SECTION 2.2c — Terms & Conditions (all hub types)  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Capability: dashboard_manage_terms (“Manage terms & conditions”) in Hub
    operations. Defaults ON for hub-admin style roles on Shared and White-label
    (available on Central). Edit rights via Capabilities matrix.
  - Hub stores rich HTML in hubs.terms_and_conditions JSON:
    { content, version, updated_at }. Per-hub-type default seed copy
    (Shared / White-label / Central); editable via rich-text editor.
  - On first login (and when version changes after edit), authenticated users
    must view and accept current terms before using the app.
    Stored: users.terms_accepted_at, users.terms_accepted_version.
  - APIs:
      GET/PUT /api/client-admin/terms
      POST /api/client-admin/terms/reset   (hub_can:dashboard_manage_terms)
      POST /api/auth/accept-terms         (auth)
  - Public GET /api/hub → auth.terms { required, version, content [, updated_at] }
  - Frontend: /my-dashboard/terms; TermsGate modal after login.

Actors:
  G  = Guest
  U  = Authenticated user without terms acceptance (or stale version)
  A  = Authenticated user who already accepted current version
  M  = Role WITH dashboard_manage_terms
  N  = Authenticated role WITHOUT dashboard_manage_terms

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.2c-A  Public hub payload + seeded defaults
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.2c-001 | [BE] GET /api/hub includes auth.terms
Steps: GET /api/hub as G
Expected: 200; hub.auth.terms has required, version, content
          (updated_at if exposed)

TC-2.2c-002 | [BE] terms.required true when content non-empty
Precondition: Hub has seeded/default terms HTML
Steps: GET /api/hub
Expected: auth.terms.required = true; version >= 1; content HTML non-empty

TC-2.2c-003 | [BE] terms.required false when content empty (edge)
Precondition: Force empty content if product allows
Steps: GET /api/hub
Expected: required=false; gate should not block — record: _______________

TC-2.2c-004 | [BE] Default content differs by hub type
Steps: Compare TermsAndConditionsDefaults::shared / whiteLabel / central
       (or fresh hubs of each type)
Expected: Shared mentions Shared Hub; WL white-labelled; Central control plane

TC-2.2c-005 | [BE] New hub seeds terms_and_conditions JSON
Steps: Create hub of each type; inspect DB/API
Expected: content + version + updated_at present

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.2c-B  Capability — manage terms
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.2c-006 | [BE] M can GET /api/client-admin/terms
Precondition: M has dashboard_manage_terms

SECTION 2.2d — Privacy Policy (all hub types) — UK GDPR Phase 1  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Separate from Terms (§2.2c). Terms = contractual; Privacy = UK GDPR + DPA
    2018 transparency notice.
  - Capability: dashboard_manage_privacy (“Manage privacy policy”). Defaults ON
    for hub-admin style roles (same pattern as terms); PA toggles on Central.
  - Hub stores rich HTML hubs.privacy_policy JSON { content, version, updated_at }.
    Seed defaults per hub type covering: controller, personal data, purposes,
    lawful bases, retention summary, recipients/processors (Stripe, email,
    hosting), international transfers, individual rights, contact / ICO complaint.
  - First login + version change → user must acknowledge before using app
    (same gate pattern as Terms). Stored: privacy_accepted_at,
    privacy_accepted_version.
  - GET /hub → auth.privacy { required, version, content }
  - APIs: GET/PUT /api/client-admin/privacy,
          POST /api/client-admin/privacy/reset (hub_can:dashboard_manage_privacy),
          POST /api/auth/accept-privacy (auth)
  - FE: /my-dashboard/privacy; PrivacyGate (or combined legal-gate after Terms)
  - Default Terms should shorten/remove generic Privacy subsection and point to
    Privacy Policy instead of duplicating it.
  - Full GDPR roadmap (DSAR, erasure, etc.) is §6E — out of scope here except
    Phase 1 policy notice.

Actors:
  G  = Guest
  U  = Authenticated user without current privacy acceptance
  A  = User who accepted current privacy version
  M  = Role WITH dashboard_manage_privacy
  N  = Role WITHOUT dashboard_manage_privacy
  T  = Role WITH dashboard_manage_terms only (not privacy)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.2d-A  Separation from Terms + public payload
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.2d-001 | [BE] GET /api/hub includes auth.privacy separate from auth.terms

Expected: Both auth.terms and auth.privacy present as distinct objects;
          privacy has required, version, content

TC-2.2d-002 | [BE] privacy.required true when content non-empty
Precondition: Seeded privacy policy
Steps: GET /api/hub
Expected: auth.privacy.required=true; version>=1; content non-empty

TC-2.2d-003 | [BE] Updating privacy does not change terms version/content
Precondition: terms v1; privacy v1
Steps: PUT /api/client-admin/privacy with new content as M
Expected: privacy.version=2; terms unchanged at v1

TC-2.2d-004 | [BE] Updating terms does not change privacy version/content
Steps: PUT /api/client-admin/terms as terms-capable admin
Expected: terms bumps; privacy unchanged

Expected: privacy_accepted=true; terms_accepted still false

Steps: POST /api/auth/accept-terms only
Expected: terms_accepted=true; privacy_accepted still false

SECTION 2.2e — Cookie Notice (all hub types) — UK GDPR / PECR Phase 5  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Separate from Terms and Privacy. Banner informs visitors about essential
    cookies (session + CSRF). NOT a full consent preference centre while no
    non-essential cookies exist.
  - Capability: dashboard_manage_cookies (“Manage cookie notice”). Same default
    pattern as Terms / Privacy.
  - Hub stores rich HTML hubs.cookie_notice JSON { content, version, updated_at }.
    Defaults per hub type; Save/Reset bumps version.
  - Public GET /hub → auth.cookies
    { essential_only, required, version, content, updated_at }
  - APIs: GET/PUT /api/client-admin/cookies,
          POST /api/client-admin/cookies/reset (hub_can:dashboard_manage_cookies)
  - FE: /my-dashboard/cookies; CookieBanner when localStorage version ≠ hub
    version; OK dismisses until next bump.
  - NO authenticated accept-gate (unlike Terms/Privacy) — PECR transparency
    banner is sufficient for essential-only cookies.

Actors:
  G  = Guest / any visitor
  U  = Authenticated member (banner still applies via localStorage)
  M  = Role WITH dashboard_manage_cookies
  N  = Role WITHOUT dashboard_manage_cookies

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Expected: auth.cookies object present alongside auth.terms and auth.privacy

Expected: essential_only=true

          for this phase

SECTION 2.3 — Content metrics  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Each post/reel tracks:
      reach — unique impression when listing scroll brings post into viewport
               (POST /api/posts/reach with post_ids)
      views — open post detail (GET /api/posts/{id})
      buy_count — when a user purchases the post
  - Shown on listing/detail per active subscription plan visibility flags:
      Basic (default): reach only
      Standard (default): reach + views
      Premium (default): reach + views + buys
  - Hub admins, Power Admin (acting on hub), unlimited-credit advisors → all three
  - Non-subscribers do not see metric counts
  - Plan fields: show_reach, show_views, show_buys (plan CRUD; dashboard_manage_plans)
  - API responses include visible_metrics for the frontend

APIs:
  POST /api/posts/reach   (hub_can:member_browse_catalog)
  GET  /api/posts, GET /api/posts/{id}
  Plan CRUD (show_* flags) under subscription plans
  Purchase flows increment buy_count (invoice details SKIPPED)

Actors:
  G   = Guest / no subscriber plan
  NS  = Authenticated non-subscriber (no active plan, not unlimited)
  B   = Subscriber on Basic-like plan (reach only)
  S   = Subscriber on Standard-like plan (reach + views)
  P   = Subscriber on Premium-like plan (all three)
  ADV = Unlimited-credit advisor
  HA  = Hub admin (client_admin / finproms_admin)
  PA  = Power Admin acting on hub

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.3-A  Reach — unique listing impression
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.3-001 | [BE] POST /api/posts/reach increments reach_count once per viewer
Precondition: Active post; authenticated viewer
Steps: POST /api/posts/reach { post_ids: [id] }
Expected: 200; reached_post_ids includes id; reach_count=1;
          post_reaches row viewer_key=user:{id}

TC-2.3-002 | [BE] Same viewer second reach does not double-count
Steps: POST reach same post_ids again
Expected: 200; reached_post_ids=[]; reach_count still 1; one post_reaches row

TC-2.3-003 | [BE] Different viewers each increment reach
Steps: Viewer A then Viewer B record reach on same post
Expected: reach_count=2; two post_reaches rows

TC-2.3-004 | [BE] Batch reach — multiple post_ids
Steps: POST post_ids with 3 active posts (max 50)
Expected: 200; first-time ids in reached_post_ids; each increments once

TC-2.3-005 | [BE] Validation — post_ids required, 1–50, distinct, exists
Steps: omit; empty; >50; duplicate; nonexistent id
Expected: 422 for each invalid case

TC-2.3-006 | [BE] Inactive posts ignored for reach increment
Precondition: post_ids includes inactive post
Steps: POST reach
Expected: Inactive not incremented / not in reached_post_ids

TC-2.3-007 | [BE] Reach does not increment views_count
Steps: After successful reach
Expected: views_count unchanged

TC-2.3-008 | [BE] Guest reach uses ip-based viewer_key (if endpoint allowed)
Precondition: member_browse_catalog allows guest OR auth required
Steps: POST reach as G (if permitted)
Expected: viewer_key like ip:{sha1}; unique per IP; or 401/403 if auth required
          Record: _______________

TC-2.3-009 | [BE] Reach without catalog capability denied
Steps: Authenticated user without member_browse_catalog
Expected: 403

TC-2.3-010 | [FE] Listing IntersectionObserver (or equivalent) calls reach API
Steps: Scroll catalog so tiles enter viewport
Expected: FE POSTs visible post_ids; no spam on every pixel scroll

TC-2.3-011 | [FE] Re-scrolling same tiles does not spam useless increments
Steps: Scroll away and back
Expected: API may be called but BE returns empty reached_post_ids; counts stable

================================================================================
SECTION 2.4 — Subscriptions & Credits  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Users subscribe and receive credits.
  - Credits are spent when buying posts/reels or post bundles.
  - After purchase, users can preview the content in-app (no file download).

Related product rules (capabilities / dashboard):
  - View plans / self-serve subscribe
  - One-off purchase / spend credits (detail in §2.5; credit spend covered here)
  - Preview purchased content in-app (Preview / Canva) only after a real purchase —
    hub-admin roles are NOT treated as already owning catalog items
  - Dashboard: show subscription, remaining credits, purchases
    (invoices page SKIPPED in this suite)

SKIPPED in this section:
  - Invoice generation for subscriptions / purchases (§2.6)
  - Full post-bundle CRUD (§2.4a) — only credit-spend unlock cross-check
  - One-off non-subscriber payment rails detail (§2.5) — credit path covered

APIs (primary):
  GET  /api/subscription-plans, /api/subscription-plans/{id}
  POST /api/subscription-plans/{plan}/checkout
  POST /api/subscriptions/confirm
  GET  /api/my-subscriptions
  GET  /api/my-credits
  POST /api/posts/{post}/purchase
  POST /api/bundles/{bundle}/purchase   (spend/unlock only; bundle admin in §2.4a)
  POST /api/content-purchases/confirm
  GET  /api/my-purchases
  GET  /api/posts, /api/posts/{id}      (preview / attachment visibility)

Actors:
  G   = Guest
  M   = Member with member subscribe / purchase capabilities
  SUB = Member with active paid subscription + credit balance
  LOW = Member with insufficient credits
  UNL = Unlimited-credits user (advisor) when hub allows
  HA  = Hub admin (must NOT auto-own catalog for preview)
  N   = Authenticated without purchase/subscribe caps

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.4-A  Subscribe and receive credits
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.4-001 | [BE] List active subscription plans (member)

Expected: 401

Steps: POST checkout { payment_method }

          NO credits granted until payment confirmed (unless auto-confirm)

TC-2.4-004 | [BE] Confirm paid subscription grants plan credits
Precondition: Successful payment confirm path
Steps: POST /api/subscriptions/confirm (or bank auto-confirm)

          (or credits_granted recorded); ends_at per duration_days

TC-2.4-005 | [BE] my-subscriptions lists active + recent
Steps: GET /api/my-subscriptions as SUB
Expected: 200; shows active plan and recent subscription history

Expected: Active plan info + remaining credits visible when caps allow

SECTION 2.4a — Post bundles  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Create/edit gated by dashboard_manage_bundles (hub-admin dashboard;
    Power Admin enables per role).
  - Website visibility gated by member_browse_bundles (Member catalog).
    Off for a role → Bundles nav + /bundles hidden for that role.
    Off for every role → hidden for whole hub.
    Defaults: ON Shared / White-label; OFF Central.
  - Bundle fields: title, description, total credits_cost (bundle price).
  - Creators can attach existing posts/reels and/or create new posts/reels
    in the same create/edit flow.
  - Members browse when member_browse_bundles ON; purchase spends
    bundle.credits_cost and unlocks every post in the bundle.
  - Invoices for bundle_purchase — SKIPPED in this suite (§2.6 / unclear).

APIs:
  Member:  GET /api/bundles, GET /api/bundles/{id}
           POST /api/bundles/{id}/purchase
  Admin:   GET/POST /api/bundles, PUT/POST /api/bundles/{id}, DELETE
           (hub_can:dashboard_manage_bundles)
  Acting:  /api/hub-content/bundles …

Actors:
  G  = Guest
  MB = Member WITH member_browse_bundles
  MX = Member WITHOUT member_browse_bundles
  MP = Member with browse + member_purchase_content
  AD = Role WITH dashboard_manage_bundles
  AN = Role WITHOUT dashboard_manage_bundles

SECTION 2.5 — One-off / Non-subscriber Purchases  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Users who are NOT subscribed can still buy individual posts/reels.
  - Pricing rule: content is priced in credits (no pound display in catalog copy).

Product / implementation notes (for coverage):
  - Hub functionality flag `one_off_purchase` gates cash one-off on Shared.
  - Shared may expose cash payment methods (bank_transfer / Stripe) when
    one_off_purchase ON — still priced from credits_cost.
  - White-label: credits-only buys (cash one-off rejected even if flag ON).
  - Capability: member_purchase_content for purchase APIs.
  - After purchase → in-app preview (covered in §2.4; re-check here for NS).
  - Invoice generation SKIPPED (§2.6).

APIs:
  GET  /api/posts/{id}          (credits_cost, payment_methods, flags)
  POST /api/posts/{id}/purchase
  POST /api/bundles/{id}/purchase  (NS bundle one-off where allowed)
  POST /api/content-purchases/confirm

Actors:
  G   = Guest
  NS  = Authenticated member with NO active subscription
  SUB = Authenticated member WITH active subscription (control)
  MP  = NS with member_purchase_content
  NX  = Authenticated without purchase capability
  WL  = White-label member (credits-only)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2.5-A  Non-subscribers can buy posts/reels
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-2.5-001 | [BE] NS with credits can purchase post without active subscription
Precondition: MP; credits >= post.credits_cost; hasActiveSubscription=false
Steps: POST /api/posts/{id}/purchase
Expected: 201; post.is_purchased=true; post_purchases row; credits deducted;
          still no subscription required/created

TC-2.5-002 | [BE] NS can purchase reel the same way
Precondition: Reel-type post; NS with credits
Steps: POST purchase
Expected: 201; unlocked; credits deducted by credits_cost

TC-2.5-003 | [BE] Purchase does not auto-create a subscription
Steps: After NS credit purchase; check subscriptions
Expected: No new UserSubscription; hasActiveSubscription remains false

TC-2.5-004 | [BE] SUB can also purchase (one-off path not exclusive to NS)
Steps: SUB with credits buys post
Expected: Still succeeds (control — subscribers not blocked from content buy)

TC-2.5-005 | [BE] Insufficient credits — NS purchase fails
Precondition: NS credits < cost; no cash method used
Steps: POST purchase (credits path)
Expected: 422/402; not purchased; credits unchanged

TC-2.5-006 | [BE] Guest cannot one-off purchase
Steps: POST as G
Expected: 401

TC-2.5-007 | [BE] Without member_purchase_content → 403
Steps: POST as NX
Expected: 403

TC-2.5-008 | [BE] Inactive post cannot be purchased by NS
Steps: POST purchase inactive
Expected: 404/422

Expected: Buy affordance shown; not forced to subscribe-only dead end
          (may still offer subscribe upsell)

Expected: Preview unlocked; primary UX is in-app (no file download requirement)

SECTION 2.7 — Roles & separate dashboards (+ 2.7.0 / 2.7.0a / 2.7.1 / 2.7.2 / 2.7.3)
[ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - ONE universal dashboard shell at /my-dashboard for every role; sidebar +
    home cards from Capabilities matrix (pa_* on Central for Power Admin).
  - Member catalog on main website (Shared/WL); Central optional via member_*.
  - Login lands on /my-dashboard; legacy /client-admin & /power-admin redirect.
  - Members never see tools not enabled for ANY of their roles (UNION).
  - Multi-role: many roles per user; ≥1 role required; equal roles (no primary);
    capabilities = UNION; legacy users.role = privilege-ordered mirror only.
  - Role catalog includes network_admin, firm_admin, website_admin, etc.
  - Firm vs Network scope (§2.7.0) + Delegated compliance access (§2.7.0a).
  - Separation of duties (§2.7.2); per-user capability overrides (§2.7.3).

SKIPPED: Import Users Excel role-column format details (import suite deferred).

Actors:
  PA = power_admin
  FA = finproms_admin (Shared)
  CA = client_admin (WL)
  NA = network_admin (central firm)
  FM = firm_admin (member firm)
  WA = website_admin
  TM = top_management
  AA = accounts_admin
  AU = auditor
  MG = manager
  AP = approver
  AD = advisor
  U  = user
  AS = admin_staff
  MR = multi-role user (e.g. manager+approver)

================================================================================
SECTION 3.3 — Social Media Compliance  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Note: §3.1 AI Content Generation and §3.2 In-app Post Editing are marked
“later” in requirements → DEFERRED (no cases until product asks).

Requirement summary:
  - After purchase (and later editing), posts can go through compliance check.
  - Power Admin enables **Social Media Pre Approval Workflow** module per hub.
    Website modules are separate (§3.3d).
  - While module OFF: all smc_* capabilities blurred/inactive on matrix.
  - Who can submit/assign/review/view/report = Capabilities matrix only
    (not hard-coded Approver/buyer roles).
  - Flow: buyers submit purchased post (description + image) → managers assign
    → reviewers set Pending / Approved / Rejected / Approved with Feedback;
    version history for reject-resubmit and approve-with-feedback confirm/reupload.
  - Emails notify admins, assignees, submitters.
  - Optional supporting_files[] (PDF, Office, images, ZIP; max 10 × 10 MB) on
    submit / review / change-status; copied across versions when none uploaded.
  - UI: supporting files ONLY inside versions[].supporting_files (not request-level
    box); each file has source, uploaded_by_name, uploaded_by_role
    (advisor|approver|manager).
  - activity_logs: smc.submit / assign / unassign / review / resubmit /
    confirm_feedback / change_status / reports.* / charts.*
  - compliance_audit_events per request with actor name/email/role, related user,
    from→to status, version, description; detail + reports expose audit_trail[]
    and CSV audit_trail_summary.

Capabilities (blurred when module off):
  smc_submit_request, smc_view_own_requests, smc_assign_requests,
  smc_review_requests, smc_change_request_status, smc_view_all_requests,
  smc_view_reports

APIs (representative):
  POST /api/social-media-compliance/requests
  POST .../resubmit, .../confirm-feedback
  GET  .../requests/mine, .../requests/{id}
  GET  .../requests, .../queue
  POST .../review, .../assign, .../change-status
  GET  .../reviewers, .../reports, .../reports/export, .../charts/*

Actors:
  SUB = purchaser with smc_submit_request
  MG  = manager-style (assign / change-status caps)
  AP  = reviewer with smc_review_requests
  RP  = report viewer smc_view_reports
  X   = user without relevant smc_* caps
  PA  = Power Admin (module toggle)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================================
SECTION 3.3b — General Compliance  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Same review flow as SMC (submit → assign → review → versions / reports),
    but free-form: required description + optional attachments[] (primary) and
    optional supporting_files[] (evidence); max 10 × 10 MB each field.
  - attachments[] and supporting_files[] are DISTINCT (kind on each file row).
    Do not alias one into the other.
  - Supporting files: advisor on submit/resubmit/confirm; approver on review;
    manager on change-status (appended to current/new version).
  - UI: supporting files ONLY in versions[].supporting_files; primary
    attachments may show with request content; no separate supporting panel.
  - Module: General Compliance; gc_* caps blurred while module off.
  - activity_logs gc.*; compliance audit trail module=gc.
  - Encryption at rest (NFR-GC-ENC): description, feedback, future_feedback via
    SafeEncrypted; list search q matches name / content type / reviewer only;
    audit stores feedback presence flags only; artisan backfill command.
  - Attachment files on public disk with public file_url (file-byte encryption
    reverted). Authenticated /api/gc/file/{id} fallback only for is_encrypted=true
    legacy rows.

Capabilities:
  gc_submit_request, gc_view_own_requests, gc_assign_requests, gc_review_requests,
  gc_change_request_status, gc_view_all_requests, gc_view_reports,
  gc_manage_content_types, gc_download_attachments

Actors:
  SUB = submitter with gc_submit_request
  MG  = assign / change-status
  AP  = review
  CT  = gc_manage_content_types
  RP  = gc_view_reports
  X   = no relevant gc_* 

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.3b-A  Module gate & matrix
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-3.3b-001 | [BE/FE] Module OFF → gc_* blurred/inactive
Steps: Open Capabilities with General Compliance off
Expected: All gc_* blurred; APIs reject mutations

TC-3.3b-002 | [BE] Module ON → gc_* enforceable
Steps: Enable module; grant caps; hit APIs
Expected: Caps work; APIs succeed when permitted

TC-3.3b-003 | [BE] Rights matrix-driven (not hard-coded Approver)
Steps: Grant gc_review_requests to non-approver role
Expected: That role can review

TC-3.3b-004 | [FE] GC nav hidden without module/caps
Steps: Login various roles
Expected: GC tools only when module ON + relevant gc_*

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.3b-B  Free-form submit — description + attachments vs supporting files
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-3.3b-005 | [BE] Submit with required description only
Precondition: SUB; module ON
Steps: POST /api/general-compliance/requests { description, content_type? }
Expected: 201; request created; no purchased-post requirement (unlike SMC)

TC-3.3b-006 | [BE] Description required — empty rejected
Steps: POST without description / empty
Expected: 422

TC-3.3b-007 | [BE] Optional attachments[] stored as primary (kind=attachment)
Steps: POST multipart attachments[] (1–10 valid files ≤10MB)
Expected: 201; files returned separately from supporting_files; kind distinct

TC-3.3b-008 | [BE] Optional supporting_files[] stored as evidence (kind=supporting)
Steps: POST with supporting_files[] only or with attachments
Expected: Stored separately; not aliased into attachments

TC-3.3b-009 | [BE] Max 10 files × 10 MB per field enforced
Steps: 11 attachments; oversized file
Expected: 422 each case

TC-3.3b-010 | [BE] Invalid types rejected for both fields
Steps: Disallowed mime/extension
Expected: 422

TC-3.3b-011 | [BE] Without gc_submit_request → 403
Steps: POST as X
Expected: 403

TC-3.3b-012 | [BE] Content type from GC content-types catalog when provided
Steps: Submit with valid content_type name/id
Expected: Accepted; invalid type 422 if validated

TC-3.3b-013 | [BE] activity_logs gc.submit (+ audit trail submit)
Steps: After submit
Expected: gc.* activity; compliance_audit_events module=gc with actor info

SECTION 3.3d2 — Live Chat (hub users ↔ Central Hub agents)  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Functionalities flag `live_chat` (NOT a Modules billable package).
    Available on Shared, White-label, and Central.
  - Content-hub users message selected Central users; client polling ~4s;
    no websockets.
  - Central contacts picker on Shared/WL ONLY: /my-dashboard/live-chat/access
    — NOT on Central. Lists ALL Central Hub users (via internal API or local
    Central DB when PA acts remotely). Stored as hubs.live_chat_agents JSON
    (id, name, email). Members may only chat those contacts.
  - Also sets hubs.live_chat_support_email (default support@fin-proms.com);
    notice at top of Live Chat on content hubs.
  - Storage on each content hub DB: live_chat_conversations + messages.
    Central agents R/W via remote DB when listed. 1 hub user ↔ 1 Central agent.
    Conversations stay open (no end/close).
  - Caps (blurred while live_chat off):
      lc_use_chat — Shared/WL only (inactive on Central matrix)
      lc_manage_access — Shared/WL only; grants agent inbox by being checked
  - APIs: options, unread, conversations CRUD/messages, access GET/PUT,
    internal central-users
  - Activity: live_chat.start / message / access_updated
  - FE: /my-dashboard/live-chat (+ /access on content hubs)

Actors:
  HU = Hub user with lc_use_chat (Shared/WL)
  MA = Hub admin with lc_manage_access
  CA = Central user listed in live_chat_agents for a content hub
  CX = Central user NOT listed
  X  = User without lc_* / functionality off

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.3d2-A  Functionality gate (not a Module)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Expected: live_chat under Functionalities; not billed as product module package

Steps: Open matrix; POST conversation as HU
Expected: Caps blurred; 403 on use/manage APIs

SECTION 3.3e — Support Tickets (error / bug reporting)  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Functionalities flag `support_tickets` (NOT a Modules product module).
    Available on Shared, White-label, Central.
  - Human tickets (st_submit_ticket) + separate automatic system_error_reports
    (do NOT auto-create tickets).
  - System errors: FE ErrorBoundary + window.onerror/unhandledrejection →
    POST /api/system-errors (auth, throttle, fingerprint dedupe); BE Laravel
    reportable (skips 4xx/validation/auth/throttle/system-errors route).
    Staff UI /system-errors: Acknowledge / Resolve / Ignore / Reopen + note
    (st_view_system_errors).
  - Ticket fields: subject, module, category, urgency (Normal=medium / High=high),
    remarks; optional page URL, browser info, screenshots[] (images/PDF ≤5MB or
    short video ≤25MB; max 8). In-app capture screenshot / record screen.
  - Statuses: Open → In Progress → Waiting on User → Resolved → Closed
  - Caps (blurred while support_tickets off): st_submit_ticket, st_view_own_tickets,
    st_view_all_tickets (read-only), st_reply_to_tickets (sole staff action),
    st_view_reports, st_view_system_errors. Retired: st_change_ticket_status,
    st_comment_on_tickets (merged into reply).
  - Staff reply: required status + remarks; optional ETA + attachments.
  - Reports with filters/charts/CSV; submitter.profile_detailed for queue staff.
  - Activity: st.submit / reply / change_status / comment / reports.* / charts.* /
    system_errors.update_status

Actors:
  SU = submitter (st_submit_ticket + view own)
  VW = st_view_all_tickets only (read-only queue)
  RP = st_reply_to_tickets
  RR = st_view_reports
  SE = st_view_system_errors
  X  = no st_* / functionality off

SECTION 3.3f — Security Monitoring (+ §6F)  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary (3.3f + 6F):
  - Functionalities flag `security_monitoring` (NOT one of 6 Modules packages).
    Available Central / Shared / WL. Default OFF. While off: secmon_* blurred;
    nav hidden; APIs 403.
  - Purpose: security events/incidents report (when/what/severity/source/status).
  - Distinct from Activity Logs+anomalies (6A), Compliance audit (6C), Support
    Tickets (3.3e), GDPR ICO runbook (6E.3).
  - Caps: secmon_view_events, secmon_view_report, secmon_manage_incidents,
    secmon_manage_alerts, secmon_manage_disaster_recovery,
    secmon_manage_access. Defaults ON hub-admin style; OFF for member roles.
  - Events catalogue A–E (auth, authorization, data/secrets/FW, dependency,
    manual). Incidents roll up events. Required detail fields; never store
    passwords/tokens/secrets/card data.
  - UI: /my-dashboard/security-monitoring — Events / Incidents / Report;
    healthy empty state; Monitor now (scan-now).
  - APIs for events, incidents, report, alert-settings, scan-now, access-control,
    disaster-recovery (module-scoped — NOT whole-hub restore).
  - Continuous detectors; optional high+ alerts; GDPR retention/prune/anonymise.

Actors:
  VE = secmon_view_events
  VR = secmon_view_report
  MI = secmon_manage_incidents
  MA = secmon_manage_alerts
  MD = secmon_manage_disaster_recovery
  AC = secmon_manage_access
  X  = no secmon_* / flag off

SECTION 3.3c — Website Content Pre Approval supporting files (+ WC audit trail)
[ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - WC change requests accept optional `supporting_files[]` on:
      • submit (advisor/editor)
      • approve / reject / approve-with-feedback / schedule (approver)
      • change-status (manager)
    Same limits as other compliance modules (PDF/Office/images/ZIP; max 10 ×
    10 MB typical — match SMC/GC product limits).
  - Stored per change-request **version** with attribution:
    `source`, `uploaded_by_*`, `uploaded_by_role`.
  - UI: supporting files ONLY inside each version in version history —
    never a separate request-level supporting-files box.
  - Compliance audit trail `module=wc` for submit / assign / review / reject /
    schedule / publish / resubmit / confirm_feedback / change_status with
    actor name/email/role. Detail includes `audit_trail[]`.
  - Platform report: GET …/website-compliance/reports/change-requests for
    full history rows.

Note: Full Template Library vs Content Pre Approval module split is §3.3d.
      These cases assume Content Pre Approval module is ON and user has the
      relevant wc_* caps for each action.

Actors:
  ED = editor/advisor with wc_submit_change_requests (and edit as needed)
  AP = approver with wc_review_change_requests
  MG = manager with wc_change_request_status
  VW = viewer with report / view-all caps
  X  = no relevant wc_*

SECTION 3.3d — Website Template Library vs Website Content Pre Approval
[ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  Formerly one “Website Compliance” product → TWO Modules checklist items.
  Content Pre Approval depends on Template Library.
  Shared API namespace `/api/website-compliance/*`; module + capability gates
  decide which half is available.
  FE shows TWO nav sections (not one umbrella).

  A) Website Template Library (`module_website_template_library`)
     Caps group “Website Template Library” (blurred when module off):
       wc_manage_templates, wc_request_deployments, wc_assign_website_templates,
       wc_request_go_live, wc_view_all_deployments, wc_deploy_websites
     - PA manages templates (+ color_schemes JSON, available_pages JSON)
     - Capable users browse catalogue; request deploy (wc_request_deployments)
       with domain/branding/colours + extras (services, images, contact_details,
       policies, selected_pages, page_contents — file attachments, not textareas)
     - Colour: pick template scheme OR custom primary/secondary → stored on
       request as primary_color / secondary_color
     - Network Admin / Firm Admin preferred path: request + assign Website Admins
       (multi-select website_admin_ids; legacy assigned_advisor_id); pivot
       wc_template_request_website_admins; first id → assigned_advisor_id
       Eligibility = Delegated compliance access (§2.7.0a);
       GET …/website-admins
     - website_scope auto: network | firm | independent (read-only UI checkboxes)
     - request-go-live: original requester + wc_request_go_live → ready_for_live;
       PA promotes with wc_deploy_websites
     - Deploy never automatic; PA with wc_deploy_websites (credentials + domain)

  B) Website Content Pre Approval (`module_website_compliance`)
     Requires Template Library ON (module dependency).
     Caps group “Website Content Pre Approval” (blurred when module off):
       wc_edit_sections, wc_submit_change_requests, wc_assign_change_requests,
       wc_view_all_change_requests, wc_review_change_requests,
       wc_change_request_status, wc_manage_deployment_sections,
       wc_publish_live_content, wc_view_activity_logs, wc_view_platform_report
     - Edit/lock sections → submit CRs
     - Assign → review: approve&publish (→ published) OR approve only (→ approved
       → schedule → scheduled → published) / reject / approve-with-feedback
     - Status overrides blocked once published or scheduled
     - Optional bypass: wc_publish_live_content
     - Supporting files: §3.3c

APIs (representative):
  Templates CRUD; GET catalogue
  POST/GET template-requests; assign website admins; request-go-live; deploy
  GET website-admins
  Sections lock/edit; change-requests submit/assign/review/schedule/change-status
  Publish-live bypass; activity logs; platform report

Actors:
  PA = Power Admin (manage templates / deploy / module toggles)
  NA = Network Admin (request network site + assign WAs)
  FA = Firm Admin (request firm site + assign WAs)
  WA = Website Admin (edit/submit after deploy)
  AD = Advisor (independent self-serve request / editor)
  AP = Approver (wc_review_change_requests)
  MG = Manager (wc_change_request_status / assign)
  PL = Publish-live bypass user (wc_publish_live_content)
  X  = No relevant wc_* 

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.3d-A  Module split, dependency, nav
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-3.3d-001 | [BE] Two distinct module keys on checklist
Steps: Inspect hub modules
Expected: module_website_template_library AND module_website_compliance both exist

TC-3.3d-002 | [BE] Content Pre Approval depends on Template Library
Steps: Enable compliance while Template Library off (or disable Template Library
       while compliance on)

================================================================================
SECTION 3.4 — Document Repository (Firm Documents)
[ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  Product: Document Repository (UI may say “Firm documents” / “Documents”).
  Firm-scoped library on Shared / White-labelled hubs (own firms/members).
  Heads via roles (preferred) and/or Assign Head UI; per-member rights.

  3.4.0 Functionality gate `firm_documents` (hub-wide matrix only)
    - Default OFF; PA enables on Functionalities (often Central → sync to hub DB)
    - While OFF: hub-wide firm_documents_* caps blurred
    - Head role unlocks Document Repository for THAT firm without the flag
    - dashboard_assign_firm_head NOT gated by this flag

  3.4.1 Heads
    - network_admin = Head of Central / Network firm only (firm_id = central;
      multiple OK)
    - firm_admin = Head of own member firm (multiple OK); NOT for central firm
    - Must NOT hold both network_admin and firm_admin
    - Firm-scoped by membership; multi-role UNION with other roles OK
    - Firm change while holding head role: move/clear consistently OR reject

  3.4.1a Appointing (two sync paths)
    Path 1: dashboard_assign_firm_head → head_user_ids[]; sync roles;
            firms.head_user_id = optional primary (first / retained)
    Path 2: Role assign network_admin/firm_admin — adds without revoking others;
            head_user_id only if empty; reject unqualified firm membership
    is_firm_head = matching head role + firm OR legacy head_user_id / same-email
    Legacy “any role as head” superseded; migrate from head_user_id

  3.4.2 Head rights (own firm): ALL doc rights immediately — add/view/delete/
    archive, grant member rights, visible-firms allowlist — no matrix / no flag.
    Network Admin own-firm = central only (NOT automatic hub-wide).
    Hub-wide ALL firms needs §3.4.4 matrix ticks.

  3.4.3 Per-document member grants (key icon): add/view/delete/archive combos;
    can_add also unlocks firm-level upload; cleared on leave/discontinue.

  3.4.3a Folders/subfolders firm-scoped; unfiled separate; upload above table.
  3.4.3b Categories firm_document_categories; firm_documents_manage_categories
    blurred while firm_documents OFF; optional on upload.
  3.4.3c Taxonomy requests (taxonomy_request_add / _manage) cover firm doc
    categories when functionality ON (+ SM/GC as applicable).

  3.4.4 Hub-wide caps (editable when firm_documents ON) for EVERY role column
    including NA/FA columns (defaults OFF for hub-wide on those columns):
    firm_documents_view|add|delete|archive|manage_access_rights|
    manage_categories|manage_firm_access

  3.4.4a Visible firms allowlist + mixed per-doc rights (own members +
    allowlisted firms); Shared from other firms; remove firm clears grants.

  3.4.5 activity_logs for head assign/replace/clear, roles, member/firm rights,
    visible_firms, add/folder/delete/archive/(optional view).

APIs (representative):
  Assign head / role sync; firm-documents CRUD; folders; categories;
  member rights; GET/PUT visible-firms; firm rights; taxonomy-add-requests;
  activity_logs

Actors:
  PA = Power Admin (functionality / matrix / assign head)
  NA = Network Admin (central firm head)
  FA = Firm Admin (member firm head)
  M  = Firm member (non-head)
  HW = Hub-wide operator with firm_documents_* matrix caps
  TX = Taxonomy requester / manager
  X  = Outsider / wrong firm / no rights
