================================================================================
SECTION 4.1 — Multi-instance / Same Codebase + Separate Databases  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Central Hub Controller = registry + remote-control source for many Shared
    and White-labelled hubs (same operational model; owned by Central).
  - ONE codebase artefact for Central, Shared hub(s), and all White-labels;
    EACH instance has its OWN database (not multi-tenant shared content DB).
  - Per-hub differences (branding, page copy, dashboard menu, etc.) driven by
    that hub’s DB — not code forks.
  - Hub settings split into THREE capability-gated dashboard pages:
      1) General settings — dashboard_manage_settings
         FE /my-dashboard/settings
         API GET/PUT /api/client-admin/settings
         (app name, from-email, new_banner_days, logos, favicon, auth bg,
          colour scheme)
      2) Website content — dashboard_manage_page_content
         FE /my-dashboard/page-content
         API GET/PUT /api/client-admin/page-content
         + GET import-sources / POST import { source_hub_id }
         (Home / Posts·Reels catalog / post detail + footer powered-by logo)
      3) Dashboard menu — dashboard_manage_dashboard_nav
         FE /my-dashboard/dashboard-menu
         API GET/PUT /api/client-admin/dashboard-nav
         + GET import-sources / POST import { source_hub_id }
  - Values exposed on public GET /api/hub under branding / page_content /
    dashboard_nav. Caps gated independently on the Capabilities matrix.
  - page_content on hubs.page_content (JSON); defaults PageContentDefaults;
    Posts+Reels share catalog page; placeholders {credits}{date}{count}{year}
    {brand} substituted on FE.
  - Public Home `/` sections: Hero, Browse by Categories (top 8), Website
    templates (always 3 cards / omit if none or WC off), Support tickets
    (3 tabs), Documents promo, Features (4 cards); footer copyright + Powered
    by; header brand + nav_tagline + site links.
  - dashboard_nav on hubs.dashboard_nav (JSON); defaults DashboardNavDefaults;
    hub-type separator labels; section_order / item_groups / item_order /
    custom separators.
  - Central registry stores hub_type, frontend_url, api_url, db_* (password
    encrypted), deploy_notes; ready = active + frontend_url + remote DB creds;
    badges Wiring ready / Needs wiring; hub detail = wiring only (Functionalities
    / Modules / Capabilities / credits via Control hub switcher). Deploy contract:
    same codebase + own .env (DB_* + FRONTEND_URL/APP_URL); HUB_SLUG identifies
    hub; frontend_url preferred for emails/Stripe return URLs on that deploy.
  - When Central acts on a content hub, relative media paths resolve against
    that hub’s api_url / frontend_url media base (not Central APP_URL).
  - Content push / remote acting uses stored remote DB (or API) into the target
    hub’s own database (detail also §4.4).

Cross-ref: §1 / §1A cover hub types + registry smoke. This section deepens
  settings split, page content, Home sections, dashboard nav, deploy wiring,
  and multi-instance isolation for White-label + additional Shared hubs.

Actors:
  G   = Guest / public visitor
  M   = Authenticated member (no settings caps)
  S   = Role WITH dashboard_manage_settings
  PC  = Role WITH dashboard_manage_page_content
  DN  = Role WITH dashboard_manage_dashboard_nav
  PA  = Power Admin on Central (pa_manage_hubs / control plane)
  CA  = Client Admin on a content hub

Layer tags:
  [BE] = backend API / data
  [FE] = frontend UX (hub-finproms-frontend)
  [FE+BE] = both
  [PROCESS] = ops / deploy checklist

APIs / surfaces in scope:
  GET  /api/hub  (branding, page_content, dashboard_nav, …)
  GET/PUT /api/client-admin/settings
  GET/PUT /api/client-admin/page-content
  GET  /api/client-admin/page-content/import-sources
  POST /api/client-admin/page-content/import
  GET/PUT /api/client-admin/dashboard-nav
  GET  /api/client-admin/dashboard-nav/import-sources
  POST /api/client-admin/dashboard-nav/import
  GET  /api/website-compliance/public/templates  (Home templates section)
  Power Admin hub registry / deploy wiring APIs (Central)
  FE: /, /posts?type=post|reel, /my-dashboard/settings,
      /my-dashboard/page-content, /my-dashboard/dashboard-menu

Next: §4.2 Private Access (White-labelled) (below)

================================================================================
SECTION 4.2 — Private Access (White-labelled)  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - White-labelled hubs are PRIVATE: public users cannot subscribe.
  - Client supplies an Excel sheet of advisors; those advisors are added into
    the application (Import Users).
  - Power Admin (Central) sets subscriber credits for the hub via dashboard +
    Capabilities key `dashboard_manage_subscriber_credits`:
      • Unlimited (default for white-label), OR
      • Fixed credits per subscriber per billing period.
  - On Excel import, each new / reactivated advisor receives that allotment
    (unlimited flag, or balance set to the hub credit number).
  - If PA changes the credit number (or unlimited ↔ fixed) after advisors are
    already subscribed, existing balances are NOT changed mid-period; the new
    allotment applies on the next monthly autorenew to all active advisors.
  - Client admin (or PA with import capability) can Discontinue an individual
    advisor: permanent end of access (WP FPSUB end equivalent). Discontinued
    advisors cannot log in, drop out of billing counts, and are NOT restored
    when the hub flips private again. Re-importing the same email restores
    them. Cap: `advisor_discontinue`. UI on Hub → Users
    (`/my-dashboard/hub-users`), not on Import Users.
  - Import Users page (`/my-dashboard/advisors`) capability split:
      advisor_excel_template  — download blank Excel template
      advisor_excel_submit    — upload filled sheet → Pending in history
      advisor_excel_import    — import immediately OR process a Pending
                                submission from history
      advisor_excel_view_submissions — see ALL submissions + download stored
                                file (Submit-only roles see own only)
      History: who / when / file / user count / created / updated / skipped
  - private → public (Functionalities): all Excel-imported advisors SUSPENDED
    (not deleted); cannot log in; lose unlimited/subscription access; excluded
    from advisor billing counts; Advisor Stripe auto-renew canceled; pending
    advisor billings canceled; private-related funcs OFF / public ON
    (paid_credits, one_off_purchase, public_subscribe ON; unlimited_credits +
    advisor_subscriber_billing OFF).
  - public → private: previously suspended imported advisors REACTIVATED with
    current subscriber-credit allotment + advisor_import subscriptions restored;
    auto-renew is NOT restarted automatically (CA pays again via import/billing
    if needed).

Note: Master file still lists a deep “Import Users Excel column-format” suite as
  SKIPPED. This section covers the §4.2 private-access + credit + discontinue +
  visibility-transition + Import Users *capability workflow* as specified here.
  Column-schema edge cases remain deferred unless you ask to expand them.

Actors:
  G   = Guest / public visitor
  A   = Excel-imported advisor (active)
  S   = Suspended imported advisor (private→public cascade)
  D   = Discontinued advisor
  CA  = Client Admin on WL hub
  PA  = Power Admin on Central (acting on WL hub when needed)
  T   = Role with advisor_excel_template only
  Sub = Role with advisor_excel_submit only
  Imp = Role with advisor_excel_import
  View= Role with advisor_excel_view_submissions (not Import)
  SC  = Role with dashboard_manage_subscriber_credits
  Disc= Role with advisor_discontinue
  N   = Authenticated user without the relevant private caps

Layer tags:
  [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS]

APIs / surfaces in scope:
  Auth register / subscribe (must be blocked when private)
  GET/PUT /api/client-admin/subscriber-credits
      (hub_can:dashboard_manage_subscriber_credits)
  GET  /api/client-admin/advisors
  GET  /api/client-admin/advisors/template
  POST /api/client-admin/advisors/submit
  POST /api/client-admin/advisors/import
  GET  /api/client-admin/advisors/import/{jobId}
  GET  /api/client-admin/advisors/import-history
  GET  /api/client-admin/advisors/import-submissions/{batch}/download
  POST /api/client-admin/advisors/import-submissions/{batch}/import
  POST /api/client-admin/advisors/{advisor}/discontinue
  Hub users UI /api/client-admin/hub-users* (Discontinue surface)
  Functionalities checklist update (private_invite_only ↔ public_subscribe)
  FE: /my-dashboard/advisors, /my-dashboard/hub-users,
      /my-dashboard/subscriber-credits (or equivalent)

Checklist / mode flags (HubVisibilityTransitionService):
  PRIVATE_MODE: private_invite_only=true, public_subscribe=false,
    unlimited_credits=true, paid_credits=false, one_off_purchase=false,
    advisor_subscriber_billing=true
  PUBLIC_MODE:  public_subscribe=true, private_invite_only=false,
    paid_credits=true, unlimited_credits=false, one_off_purchase=true,
    advisor_subscriber_billing=false

Next: §4.3 Module recurring pricing for White-labelled (below)
================================================================================

================================================================================
SECTION 4.3 — Module recurring pricing for White-labelled  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Advisor pricing tiers are RETIRED. Recurring charges use module catalogue +
    slot tables (editable under Modules → Module prices).
  - Functionality: `charge_recurring_per_module`
      default ON white-label / OFF shared.
  - Slot tables for seat modules:
      #1 SMTL (Social Media Template Library)
      #2 SMC  (Social Media Pre Approval / Compliance)
      #3 GC   (Generic Compliance)
    Bands by active user headcount: 0–100 / 101–250 / 251–500 / 501+.
    Each band: Rate + network margin.
  - Import Option B:
      • Rate from TOTAL active users with that module (not suspended,
        not discontinued)
      • Invoice charges RATE × THIS IMPORT BATCH only
      • Due today
  - Same batch renews monthly on the import anniversary day (active users only).
  - Renew day (`/my-dashboard/advisor-renewal`): charge hub payment card for
    ALL due unpaid invoices (one-time + recurring).
    Grace day (≥ renew day): after grace,
      • unpaid recurring seat invoices → suspend those users
      • unpaid one-time module invoices → disable those modules
  - Discontinue (`advisor_discontinue`) applies to ANY hub user (not only
    advisors); discontinued users excluded from future recurring seat counts.

Cross-ref: Fuller catalogue defaults, units, packages, who-pays settings live in
  §5.1B / §5.1B2. This section covers the §4.3 WL recurring-seat model.
  Deep invoice document/PDF assertions remain SKIPPED (master file §2.6 /
  module invoices skip) — billing behaviour / amounts / status are in scope.

Actors:
  PA  = Power Admin (module pricing / functionalities)
  CA  = Client Admin on WL hub
  MP  = Role with dashboard_manage_module_pricing
  Ren = Role with dashboard_manage_advisor_renewal (renew+grace dates)
  Imp = Role that can import advisors/users
  Disc= Role with advisor_discontinue
  U   = Active hub user with a seat module
  S   = Suspended user
  D   = Discontinued user
  N   = Role without relevant caps

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS]

APIs / surfaces in scope:
  Functionalities: charge_recurring_per_module
  GET/PUT  /api/.../module-pricing  (hub_can:dashboard_manage_module_pricing)
  POST/PUT/DELETE module-recurring-tiers
  FE /my-dashboard/module-pricing  (Module prices; slot picker)
  Advisor import → module seat invoice creation (Option B)
  GET/PUT advisor-billing-renewal  (renew day + grace day)
  FE /my-dashboard/advisor-renewal  (“Billing renew day”)
  Scheduler: billing:process-module-cycles (anniversary, renew charge, grace)
  POST discontinue (any hub user)
  (Advisor-pricing page removed from nav)

Default seat module keys (code):
  module_social_media_template_library  → slot #1
  module_social_media_compliance        → slot #2
  module_general_compliance             → slot #3

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
4.3-A  Advisor pricing tiers retired → module catalogue + slots
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Next: §4.4 Remote control of content hubs (below)

================================================================================
SECTION 4.4 — Remote control of content hubs (Central Hub Controller)  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Cap `dashboard_control_white_label_hubs` (“Control hubs remotely”) on CENTRAL
    — replaces old per-form Hub dropdown / `dashboard_push_content`.
  - Hub switcher at top of Central dashboard for users with that cap.
  - Central context → platform tools only (registry, checklists, …).
  - Shared/WL selected → menus/tools follow THAT hub’s Capabilities matrix for
    the user’s role; authenticated GET /hub overlays that hub’s dashboard_nav.
    Creating posts/types/categories/tags is Central-only (Central library);
    while a content hub is selected those pages are list-only. Bundles + other
    hub tools still write to THAT hub’s own DB when enabled.
  - “Website” / “Back to website” always opens THIS deploy’s `/`, gated by
    `member_view_site_pages` on the Central (control-plane) matrix — NOT the
    selected hub’s frontend_url. PA/FinProms Admin member_view_site_pages
    cells always read/save on Central even while switcher is on a content hub.
  - APIs: GET/PUT /client-admin/acting-hub (+ power-admin/admin aliases).
    Authenticated GET /hub includes hub_switcher + acting effective caps when
    on Central with remote-control cap.
  - Switcher target: active + remote DB credentials (deploy wiring). Does NOT
    require `receive_content_from_shared`.
  - `receive_content_from_shared` gates Central library distribute / content
    push only — not the switcher.
  - Central content library (`dashboard_central_content_library`, Central only):
      Create manual posts one-by-one or Excel bulk (template like advisor
      import); posts stay in Central DB. AI tab = empty stub.
      Distribute non-archived (ready) posts → insert copies on target hub DB;
      re-distribute skips duplicates (content_pushes + remote row).
      Archive (optional): retires from distribute pool with required remarks;
      stays on Central list. Unarchive clears remarks → back in pool.
      Central posts list `/my-dashboard/posts` (ready+archived, search/filters,
      “published on” hubs). Edit Central post also updates distributed copies.
      Tabs: Create / AI posts / Distribute / Archive (Archive own tab).
      Distribute checks: non-archived only; AI→hubs with ai_posts; manual→
      manual_posts. Targets = every active Shared+WL; eligible need Receive +
      remote DB + SMTL + Manual or AI; ineligible visible+disabled+reason.
      WL defaults receive + manual posts (same as Shared).
  - Taxonomy: Central own tables; create upserts names into Central; distribute
    upserts by name into target; Central Manage categories CRUD syncs to all
    eligible hubs (soft-fail per hub, hub_sync summary); copy not live link;
    switcher on content hub → Manage taxonomy writes THAT hub only; public
    taxonomy GETs need member_browse_catalog OR Central library / manage-* caps.
  - Remote media stored as public URL from Central/source; path on target DB.
  - Acting hub: normal `/posts|/types|/categories|/tags|/bundles` or
    `/hub-content/...` — no hub_id; IDs are remote DB IDs.
  - Shared no longer hosts cross-hub control plane — Central does.

Actors:
  PA  = Power Admin on Central (remote-control + library caps)
  FA  = FinProms Admin on Central
  RC  = Role WITH dashboard_control_white_label_hubs
  NL  = Role WITH dashboard_central_content_library
  N   = Authenticated without remote-control / library caps
  G   = Guest

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS]

APIs / surfaces in scope:
  GET/PUT /api/client-admin/acting-hub (aliases under power-admin/admin)
  GET /api/hub  → hub_switcher, effective caps, dashboard_nav overlay
  /api/client-admin/hub-content/*  (acting-hub scoped CRUD)
  /api/client-admin/central-library/*  (template, import, posts, archive,
      unarchive, ai stub, targets, distribute)
  /api/client-admin/content-push/*  (legacy/alternate push surfaces)
  Dashboard taxonomy + posts endpoints while acting
  FE: hub switcher, /my-dashboard/posts, Central library tabs,
      Website / Back to website

================================================================================
SECTION 4.5 — Code Updates  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Updates made in the master codebase are reflected on Central Hub, Shared
    hub(s), and White-labelled hubs (same codebase / deploy model).
  - Deploy EACH instance when releasing.

Cross-ref (do not re-test entire suites here):
  - §1B Code updates Phase 1 — tracking (/api/version, publish, refresh, badges)
  - §1C Code updates Phase 2 — apply (zips, apply selected hubs, preserve .env)
  - §4.1 Same codebase + own DB; branding/page_content/nav preserved on redeploy
  This section focuses on the multi-instance acceptance of §4.5: one master
  codebase reaches all hub types, and every deploy must be updated separately.

Actors:
  OPS = Deploy operator / release engineer
  PA  = Power Admin on Central (pa_manage_hubs)
  G   = Guest (public version endpoint)

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS]

Surfaces in scope:
  Master git/release artefact
  Per-instance deploy (Git/FTP/cPanel and/or Central Phase 2 apply)
  GET /api/version on Central + each Shared + each WL
  Central release publish / refresh / apply (smoke only; detail §1B/§1C)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

             and all White-label deploys

Expected: Same codebase lineage (no WL-only or Shared-only fork branch required)

TC-4.5-002 | [BE] Post-update behaviour differences remain checklist/DB-driven —
             not code forks
Steps: After deploying same release to Central + Shared + WL, compare type
       behaviour (control plane vs catalog vs private)
Expected: Differences follow hub type + checklists + DB settings only

TC-4.5-003 | [PROCESS] New feature shipped in master becomes available on a hub
             only after THAT hub’s instance is deployed/updated

================================================================================
SECTION 5.1 — Hub Functionalities checklist (+ §5 intro)  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary (§5 intro + §5.1):
  - Same codebase for Central / Shared / WL → behaviour differences gated by
    checklists (feature flags / rights), NOT hard-coded forks.
  - Power Admin manages checklists from CENTRAL HUB CONTROLLER for EACH Shared
    hub AND EACH White-labelled hub.
  - Opposite options must be mutually exclusive: checking one auto-unchecks the
    other (public↔private, paid↔unlimited credits, manual↔AI posts).
  - Functionalities (formerly “hub behaviour”) control tenant behaviour, e.g.:
      • public_subscribe vs private_invite_only
      • paid_credits vs unlimited_credits
      • one_off_purchase
      • receive_content_from_shared (legacy label may say “from shared”)
      • manual_posts vs ai_posts (opposites; default Manual ON) — gates which
        Central library posts this hub may receive on distribute
      • advisor_subscriber_billing
      • charge_amount_per_module — default ON WL / OFF Shared / OFF|N/A Central;
        when ON, enabling a billable product module → one-time invoice (§5.1B)
      • support_tickets (default OFF) — unlocks st_* caps (§3.3e); NOT Modules
      • live_chat (default OFF) — unlocks lc_* caps (§3.3d2); NOT Modules
      • security_monitoring (default OFF) — unlocks secmon_* (§3.3f/6F); NOT
        Modules packaging
  - Each content hub has its own configuration.
  - Edited ONLY from Hub Functionalities / hub checklist screens on Central —
    not from the Capabilities matrix (no duplication).
  - After Functionalities / Modules / Capabilities save, FE must refresh hub
    context (dashboard loader) so navbar matches new checklist + effective caps.

Cross-ref: private↔public cascades §4.2; charge_recurring §4.3; distribute gates
  §4.4; detailed feature behaviour in §3.3e/d2/f. Module toggles → §5.1A.

Actors:
  PA  = Power Admin with pa_manage_hub_checklists (Central)
  N   = User without checklist manage right
  CA  = Client Admin on content hub (must NOT edit Functionalities locally
        if product says Central-only)

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE]

APIs / surfaces:
  PUT /api/power-admin/hubs/{hub}/checklist  (pa_can:pa_manage_hub_checklists)
  GET hub admin payload / checklist_groups (Functionalities screen)
  Capabilities matrix (must NOT duplicate Functionalities editors)
  FE: Central → Functionalities for selected/wired hub; dashboard loader refresh

Next: §5.1A Modules checklist (below)

================================================================================
SECTION 5.1A — Modules checklist  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Per-hub module toggles (functionality group `modules`) on Central Modules
    screen (same hub checklist area as Functionalities, separate control):
      1) Shared Hub / White Label Hub — locked base packaging module for hub type
      2) Social Media Template Library — posts / reels catalog
      3) Social Media Pre Approval Workflow — activates SMC capability section
         (capabilities blurred while module off)
      4) Website Template Library — templates catalogue, request form, manual
         cPanel deploy (§3.3d-A); activates WTL capability section
      5) Website Content Pre Approval Workflow — section edit + CR approval
         (§3.3d-B); requires WTL; activates WC capability section (blurred off)
      6) Generic Content Pre Approval Workflow — activates GC capability section
         (blurred while off)
  - Module packages (§5.1C): optional bundles of hub base and/or the 5 product
    modules with a single package price. Product modules covered by an enabled
    package are locked (`locked_reason=module_package`) and cannot be toggled
    separately; hub base stays type-locked but is covered for billing when
    included in the package.
  - Managed via `dashboard_manage_modules` / Modules API on Central Hub.
  - When `charge_amount_per_module` ON:
      • Newly enabling a module (incl. uncheck→check again) creates a one-time
        invoice; previous enable invoices for that module are deleted so only
        the new enable invoice remains.
      • £0 catalogue prices still create a paid/complimentary invoice.
      • WTL (`billing_unit=per_website`): ONE enable invoice for currently
        deployed website template requests (`wc_template_requests`, unit rate ×
        count) — NOT showcase catalogue (`wc_templates`). New deployments also
        create a per-request one-time invoice when WTL uses per_website.
        Recurring WTL uses the same deployed request count.
      • Invoices-page backfill does not duplicate enable invoices.

Cross-ref: Functionalities charge flag §5.1; catalogue prices §5.1B; packages
  §5.1C; feature behaviour §3.3 / §3.3d. Module invoice PDF detail SKIPPED.

Actors:
  PA  = Power Admin with dashboard_manage_modules (Central / acting)
  N   = Role without manage modules
  CA  = Client Admin if granted manage modules on hub

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE]

APIs / surfaces:
  GET/PUT /api/client-admin/modules  (hub_can:dashboard_manage_modules)
  FE: Central → Modules for selected hub; capability blur on matrix
  Module billing hooks on enable (when charge_amount_per_module ON)

Module keys:
  module_shared_hub / module_white_label_hub (base, type-locked)
  module_social_media_template_library
  module_social_media_compliance
  module_website_template_library
  module_website_compliance  (depends_on: WTL)
  module_general_compliance

SECTION 5.1B — Module catalogue pricing (Modules pricing)  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Official default catalogue (editable via capability / database).
  - One-time defaults:
      Shared/WL Hub £14,500 per_network
      SMTL £0 per_network (included; invoice still created, auto-paid)
      SMC £5,000 per_network
      WTL £300 × deployed wc_template_requests (not wc_templates showcase)
      WC £10,000 per_network
      GC £3,500 per_network
  - Recurring defaults (when charge_recurring_per_module):
      Shared/WL Hub £4,500 per_network / month
      SMTL slot #1 per_user_slots / month
      SMC slot #2 per_user_slots / month
      WTL £125 per_website / month
      WC £1,500 per_firm / month
      GC slot #3 per_user_slots / month
  - Billing units (SAME dropdown for one-time billing_unit and ongoing
    recurring_billing_unit on modules AND packages; recurring also allows none):
      per_network | per_firm | per_user (fixed) | per_user_slots | per_website
    Legacy: one_time→per_network; per_adviser→per_user_slots.
  - Stored in module_pricing (+ recurring_*) and module_recurring_tiers.
  - Capabilities group “Modules pricing” (blurred while BOTH charge flags off):
      dashboard_manage_module_pricing
      dashboard_manage_module_packages → /my-dashboard/module-packages (§5.1C)
      dashboard_view_module_invoices
      dashboard_mark_module_invoices_paid
  - APIs: GET/PUT module-pricing (hub-type filtered; billing_units lists);
    POST/PUT/DELETE module-recurring-tiers (slots 1–255, min/max, rate, margin);
    module-packages…; module-invoices…
  - Invoice types: module_billing (one_time), module_recurring (recurring)
  - Scheduler: billing:process-module-cycles daily
  - FE: same unit dropdown; slot picker when per_user_slots; advisor-pricing
    nav removed; Billing renew + grace under Advisors

Note: Deep invoice PDF/line-item docs remain SKIPPED (master). Who-pays /
  separated invoice pages → §5.1B2. Packages detail → §5.1C. Seat Option B /
  renew/grace behaviour also §4.3.

Next: §5.1B2 Module invoice / payment settings (below)
================================================================================

================================================================================
SECTION 5.1B2 — Module invoice / payment settings (who pays) + separated pages
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Page `/my-dashboard/module-invoice-settings` gated by
    `dashboard_manage_module_invoice_settings`.
  - Capabilities (any role; firm/reseller caps inactive while matching setting off):
      dashboard_manage_network_payment_card — network tab on Payment card
      dashboard_manage_firm_payment_card — firm tab (needs firms_pay_own)
      dashboard_view_network_module_invoices — Module invoices (network payer)
      dashboard_view_firm_module_invoices — Module invoices (firm payer)
      dashboard_view_reseller_invoices — Reseller invoices (menu when granted;
        list fills when network_resells on)
      dashboard_manage_reseller_module_pricing — Reseller module prices
        (per_user / per_user_slots only; edit when network_resells)
      dashboard_manage_network_stripe — Network Stripe keys (save when resells)
      dashboard_mark_module_invoices_paid — settle network/firm module invoices
      Personal card: NO capability — My payment method (mandatory if
        users_pay_own OR network_resells)
  - Pages:
      /payment-card — network and/or firm cards (tabs)
      /my-payment-method — personal card (if users_pay_own or network_resells)
      /module-invoices — network/firm one-time + recurring only
      /my-invoices — personal + user-billed module invoices
      /reseller-invoices — network→user charges (admins)
      /reseller-module-pricing — amounts users pay network (only modules whose
        main Module prices unit is per_user / per_user_slots; network still
        pays FinProms at main Module prices)
      /network-stripe — publishable/secret/webhook for user payments under resells
      /one-time-invoices — ALL one-time invoices (who pays ignored)
      /advisor-invoices — label “Import users invoices”
  - Intelligent sync on save (enables only):
      network → network card + view caps
      firms_pay_own → firm card + firm view
      network_resells → reseller view
      users_pay_own / network_resells do NOT grant caps (My payment method +
        My invoices)
  - hub_module_invoice_settings:
      1) network_pays_all — network pays ALL units; clears firm + user options
      2) firms_pay_own — per_firm unit → one invoice per firm; other units network
      3) users_pay_own — per_user / per_user_slots → user pays; personal card
         mandatory; other units network
      4) network_resells — network pays platform, may re-charge users; clears
         users_pay_own; personal card still mandatory; requires reseller logo +
         invoice text (+ format standard/compact/detailed) on invoice meta;
         unlocks Reseller module prices + Network Stripe (+ sync-enables caps
         for Network / Client / Accounts Admin)
  - APIs: GET/POST/PUT module-invoice-settings (multipart for logo)
  - Billing rows: payer_scope network|firm|user + optional firm_id
  - Module invoices list: network rows need network view cap; firm rows need
    firm view + matching firm_id for firm-scoped viewers.
    Legacy dashboard_view_module_invoices alone ≈ network view — not for Firm Admin.
    Payer of record prefers users with Manage payment card + card.

Note: Invoice PDF/document detail remains SKIPPED (master). Catalogue amounts
  §5.1B; seat renew §4.3; packages §5.1C.

Actors:
  MIS = Role with dashboard_manage_module_invoice_settings
  NA  = Network / Client Admin
  FA  = Firm Admin
  AA  = Accounts Admin
  U   = Ordinary member (personal card / my invoices)
  N   = Without relevant caps

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE]

APIs / surfaces:
  GET/POST/PUT /api/.../module-invoice-settings
  /payment-card, /profile/payment-card (personal)
  /reseller-module-pricing, /network-stripe
  /module-invoices, /my-invoices, /reseller-invoices
  /one-time-invoices, /advisor-invoices

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

             dashboard_manage_module_invoice_settings
Steps: As N vs MIS
Expected: Hidden/403 without; editable with

TC-5.1B2-002 | [BE] GET/POST/PUT module-invoice-settings capability-gated

Next: §5.1C Module packages (below)

================================================================================
SECTION 5.1C — Module packages (bundled product modules + optional hub base)
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Page `/my-dashboard/module-packages` gated by
    `dashboard_manage_module_packages` (Modules pricing group).
  - Catalogue packages of Shared Hub / White Label Hub and/or the 5 product
    modules. Hub base stays type-locked on Modules page; including it in a
    package means package price covers the hub fee (no separate base invoice).
    Selecting Shared OR WL hub expands to BOTH keys (same package covers hub
    fee on Shared and WL). Central Hub Controller is never packageable.
  - Package rules:
      • ≥1 packageable module; product dependency parents auto-included
        (e.g. SMC → also SMTL). Hub base NOT auto-added (explicit only).
      • Own one-time amount + billing_unit and recurring fields (same units
        as §5.1B; recurring may be none; any slot for per_user_slots).
      • While enabled, package one-time + recurring REPLACE individual
        catalogue charges for covered modules (incl. hub base) — no double bill.
      • Packages that share any module cannot both be enabled on same hub.
  - Hub Modules page GET/PUT returns `packages` alongside `modules`.
      Enable package → modules ON + locked (locked_reason=module_package,
        locked_by_package={id,name}).
      Disable package → modules OFF unless another enabled package still covers;
        then individually toggleable. Stored as enabled_module_package_ids.
  - Billing when charge_amount_per_module ON:
      • Enable package → ONE invoice module_key=module_package:{id}, meta
        module_package_enable_invoice.
      • Covered modules skip individual one-time enables (incl. WTL per-template).
      • Re-enable supersedes prior package + covered-module enable invoices.
      • Recurring: flat package on renew day; seat packages → import/anniversary
        under module_package:{id} (users with any package module). Covered
        modules skip individual recurring.
  - Module prices page lists catalogue for acting hub type only (also §5.1B).

Cross-ref: Modules lock UI §5.1A-D; catalogue units §5.1B; who-pays §5.1B2.

Actors:
  MPk = Role with dashboard_manage_module_packages
  MM  = Role with dashboard_manage_modules (enable packages on hub)
  N   = Without caps
  PA  = Power Admin

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE]

APIs:
  GET/POST/PUT/DELETE /api/.../module-packages
  GET/PUT /api/.../modules  (packages[] + modules[])

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
5.1C-A  Page + catalogue CRUD capability
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-5.1C-001 | [FE] /my-dashboard/module-packages gated by
             dashboard_manage_module_packages

Expected: Hidden/403 without; CRUD UI with

================================================================================
SECTION 5.2 — User capabilities matrix  [ACTIVE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Power Admin manages what users can do (dashboard / member UI) for BOTH
    Shared and White-label hubs — by ROLE (matrix). Distinct from
    Functionalities (§5.1); do NOT repeat Functionalities here.
  - Role × capability matrix per hub; managed only from CENTRAL Hub Controller.
  - MULTI-ROLE (§2.7.1): effective rights = UNION (OR) across all role columns
    the user holds on that hub (then module / acting-advisor / functionality
    gates). Matrix columns remain one per role, not per user.
  - System role keys (L→R defaults): power_admin, finproms_admin, client_admin,
    network_admin, firm_admin, website_admin, top_management, accounts_admin,
    auditor, manager, approver, advisor, admin_staff, user + custom roles.
  - Matrix COLUMNS = roles held by users on hub UNION PA-added roles for that
    hub UNION must roles (power_admin, finproms_admin always). Do NOT always
    show every other system role; network_admin / firm_admin addable as dormant
    via Manage roles.
  - Manage roles (`/my-dashboard/role-display-names`,
    `dashboard_manage_role_display_names`): list roles + user counts; add dormant
    system or custom role; delete when 0 users (must roles never deletable;
    titles still renameable); rename display titles; same cap for add/delete/
    rename; per-hub columns only — custom defs platform-wide, enabling column
    per hub.
  - Member-facing, General options, Hub-admin dashboard tools, Hub users action
    icons, SMC/GC/ST/LC groups, Modules pricing, Firm documents hub-wide caps —
    as listed in PROJECT_REQUIREMENTS §5.2 (blur rules; role defaults).
  - WL client_admin / Shared finproms_admin see only what PA enables.
  - Central has its OWN §5.2 matrix — enforce in APIs + /my-dashboard nav
    (do NOT hard-code “Central = platform only” ignoring matrix).
  - Do NOT hard-code shared-vs-WL UI forks or “control plane is on Shared”.

Cross-ref: Role semantics §2.7; feature behaviour §3.3*/§3.4/§4.*; PA platform
  checklist §5.3. This section tests MATRIX management, columns, UNION, blur,
  defaults, and Central enforcement — not every feature E2E again.

Actors:
  PA  = Power Admin (matrix editor on Central)
  MR  = Role with dashboard_manage_role_display_names
  FA  = FinProms Admin (Shared)
  CA  = Client Admin (WL)
  U   = Multi-role or single-role end user
  N   = Without matrix manage rights

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE]

APIs / surfaces:
  GET/PUT /api/power-admin/capabilities/matrix
  POST /api/power-admin/capabilities/roles (add role column)
  GET/PUT /api/.../role-display-names (+ add/remove role)
  FE: Capabilities matrix; Manage roles; /my-dashboard nav driven by caps

================================================================================
SECTION 5.3 — Power Admin capabilities checklist (platform level)  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - SEPARATE from per-hub Functionalities (§5.1) and user capabilities (§5.2).
  - Controls what power_admin users can do on the Power Admin control plane
    (CENTRAL HUB CONTROLLER /power-admin tools), for example:
      • View platform dashboard
      • Manage hubs (create/list/edit Shared + White-label hubs)
      • Manage hub Functionalities and Modules checklists
      • Manage payment methods
      • Manage Power Admin / user capabilities matrix (this list + §5.2)
      • Manage users / roles (create/update/delete; assign ONE OR MORE equal
        roles — §2.7.1; no primary/secondary)
      • Manage hub backups (schedules, run/download/restore from Central)
      • Remotely control content hubs (hub switcher / acting hub)
  - Stored as platform settings on Central Hub (not per content hub).
  - Edited from Power Admin → Capabilities (Power Admin column).
  - Power Admin nav/actions and APIs must respect these flags.

Implemented pa_* keys (platform Setting power_admin_capabilities):
  pa_view_dashboard
  pa_manage_hubs
  pa_manage_hub_checklists
  pa_manage_payment_methods
  pa_manage_power_capabilities  (forced ON on save — cannot lock everyone out)
  pa_manage_users_roles
  pa_manage_hub_backups

Note: “Remotely control content hubs” in §5.3 narrative may map to Central
  matrix `dashboard_control_white_label_hubs` (§4.4 / §5.2) rather than a pa_*
  key — verify during testing (OPEN Q).

Cross-ref: Hub registry §1A/§4.1; checklists §5.1/§5.1A; matrix §5.2;
  backups §6B; remote control §4.4; PA overview §6.

Actors:
  PA  = power_admin user on Central
  N   = Non–power-admin or PA without a given pa_* flag

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE]

APIs / surfaces:
  GET /api/power-admin/capabilities/me
  PUT /api/power-admin/capabilities  (pa_can:pa_manage_power_capabilities)
  GET/PUT capabilities/matrix (user matrix — also needs pa_manage_power_capabilities)
  Hub registry / checklist / payment methods / users / backups routes each
    gated by their pa_* via pa_can middleware
  Auth/me includes power_admin_capabilities
  FE: Capabilities → Power Admin column; PA nav cards/actions

Next: §6 Power Admin (Central Hub Controller) (below)

================================================================================
SECTION 6 — Power Admin (Central Hub Controller)  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary (§6 — first block of chapter 6; reqs label is “6.” not “6.1”):
  - Role: power_admin (platform developers / us).
  - Dashboard is SEPARATE from the member app, FinProms admin, and white-label
    client_admin. PA tools live inside universal /my-dashboard gated by pa_*
    checklist (§5.3). Legacy /client-admin and /power-admin routes redirect to
    /my-dashboard.
  - Power Admin logs into the CENTRAL HUB CONTROLLER and from there manages:
      1) Hub registry (create/list Shared + White-labelled hubs, branding
         basics, hub_type, deploy wiring) — detail §1A / §4.1
      2) Hub Functionalities checklist per hub (§5.1)
      3) User capabilities matrix per hub (§5.2) — Shared AND White-label;
         multi-role UNION (§2.7.1)
      4) Power Admin’s OWN capabilities (§5.3)
      5) Platform payment methods (Stripe / bank transfer) + other platform
         settings as needed
      6) Remote hub switcher — act on Shared / White-label DBs when wired
         (§4.4)
      7) Hub backups — schedule, Backup now, list/download Central copies,
         restore (§6B detail)
      8) Module disaster recovery — via Security Monitoring (recognize →
         snapshot → roll back affected product module only) (§6B.1); distinct
         from hub backups
  - Functionalities and Capabilities must NOT duplicate each other in the UI.
  - This is the central control plane for ALL tenant behaviour and UI
    capabilities.
  - Power Admin does NOT replace FinProms admin day-to-day content work on
    Shared hubs, or client_admin work on White-labels; they configure rights
    and can remotely assist within bounds.
  - Shared Hub is no longer the Power Admin control plane.

Cross-ref: §1A / §4.1 registry+wiring; §4.4 remote; §5.1–5.3 checklists;
  §6B backups; §6B.1 module DR; §6F Security Monitoring. This section is the
  PA role / control-plane overview — deep feature behaviour stays in those
  child sections.

Actors:
  PA  = power_admin on Central (with relevant pa_* flags)
  FA  = finproms_admin on a Shared hub
  CA  = client_admin on a White-label hub
  M   = Member / non–power_admin
  X   = PA missing a given pa_* flag

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS]

APIs / surfaces:
  Auth as power_admin on Central
  /my-dashboard (universal shell; pa_* gated cards)
  Legacy /power-admin, /client-admin → redirect /my-dashboard
  Hub registry, checklists, matrix, payment methods, acting-hub, backups,
    disaster-recovery surfaces (each gated; detail in child sections)
  Auth/me includes power_admin_capabilities

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6-A  Role + separate dashboard shell
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-6-001 | [BE] power_admin is a distinct platform role
Steps: Create/login user with role power_admin on Central
Expected: Identified as PA; not conflated with finproms_admin / client_admin

TC-6-002 | [FE] PA dashboard tools live in universal /my-dashboard (not a
             separate member-catalog shell)

================================================================================
SECTION 6B — Hub Backup & Restore  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Dual storage (content hubs): each scheduled/manual backup is saved on the
    hub’s own server (storage/app/backups/{slug}/) AND a copy on Central
    (storage/app/backups/central/{slug}/).
  - Central Hub backs itself up once on the same server
    (storage/app/backups/central/{slug}/ only — no local+central duplicate).
    Power Admin can download / restore. Prefer a true off-server copy for DR.
  - Schedule is per hub, set from Central (PA + pa_manage_hub_backups):
    enabled, time (HH:MM), timezone, daily|weekly (+ weekday), retention
    local / Central. Settings sync to content hubs via remote DB push when
    wired.
  - Scheduler: `hubs:run-due-backups` every minute (requires schedule:run cron
    on each deploy). Central also triggers due remotes via hub api_url when set.
  - Restore from Central uses the Central-stored zip; content hubs receive the
    archive on POST /api/internal/hub-backups/restore (**X-Hub-Restore-Key**,
    not the backup key).
  - Purpose-scoped machine auth: compromising backup credentials MUST NOT
    authorize restore, GC file bridge, or deploy-env writes.
      • run / receive → X-Hub-Backup-Key + backup_token / HUB_BACKUP_SECRET
      • restore → X-Hub-Restore-Key + restore_token / HUB_RESTORE_SECRET
      • GC files → X-Hub-Gc-File-Key + gc_file_token / HUB_GC_FILE_SECRET
      • deploy-env → X-Hub-Deploy-Env-Key + deploy_env_token /
        HUB_DEPLOY_ENV_SECRET
      • code updates remain on X-Hub-Code-Update-Key + code_update_token
    Saving backup schedule mints all purpose tokens; WhiteLabel sync pushes
    them to content hubs. Optional HUB_INTERNAL_ALLOWED_IPS; throttle
    `internal-hub` + tighter `internal-restore`.
  - Env (optional): CENTRAL_API_URL on content hubs for independent upload;
    purpose-scoped shared secret fallbacks (never reuse one value for all);
    per-hub tokens auto-generated.
  - Capability: pa_manage_hub_backups (Power Admin platform checklist).
  - Encryption at rest (NFR-BACKUP-ENC): completed ZIPs encrypted on disk with
    streaming AES-256-GCM (`HBENC1` header) when
    HUB_BACKUP_ENCRYPT_AT_REST=true (default). Prefer shared
    HUB_BACKUP_ENCRYPTION_KEY (base64 32-byte) on Central AND every content hub.
    Without that key, key derived from local APP_KEY (single-server only).
    Legacy plaintext archives still restore/download. Download API decrypts to
    a normal ZIP for the admin. hub_backups.is_encrypted records status.
    Does not rewrite historical dumps per GDPR erasure (residual until
    retention rotates old plaintext).

Note: A later requirements block also labelled “6B” covers Active Sessions —
  that is a different feature; draft it separately after §6A / as its own
  section ID when reached.

Cross-ref: PA overview §6; pa_* gate §5.3; remote wiring §4.1/§4.4; module DR
  (§6B.1) is NOT whole-hub restore; GDPR residual risk §6E; NFR-BACKUP-ENC §9.

Actors:
  PA  = power_admin with pa_manage_hub_backups ON
  X   = PA without pa_manage_hub_backups / non-PA
  CH  = Content hub deploy (Shared / WL) with api_url + tokens
  M   = Machine / internal caller (purpose keys)

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS] [SEC]

APIs / surfaces:
  GET    /api/power-admin/hubs/{hub}/backups
  PUT    /api/power-admin/hubs/{hub}/backups/schedule
  POST   /api/power-admin/hubs/{hub}/backups                  (Backup now)
  GET    /api/power-admin/hubs/{hub}/backups/{backup}/download
  POST   /api/power-admin/hubs/{hub}/backups/{backup}/restore { confirm }
  DELETE /api/power-admin/hubs/{hub}/backups/{backup}
  POST   /api/internal/hub-backups/run
  POST   /api/internal/hub-backups/receive
  POST   /api/internal/hub-backups/restore
  Artisan: hubs:run-due-backups (scheduled every minute)
  FE: Power Admin → Hub backups (schedule, list, download, restore, delete)

      HTTP still 201 with warning (per current controller) — treat as Pass?

Next: §6B.1 Module disaster recovery and rollback (below)
================================================================================

================================================================================
SECTION 6B.1 — Module disaster recovery and rollback (via Security Monitoring)
               [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  Status: IMPLEMENTED (backend + frontend).
  Cross-refs: §6F (detect), NFR-AVAIL 9.9, §6B (whole-hub backup — DIFFERENT),
  §1C (code release apply — DIFFERENT).

  Purpose (NOT Security Monitoring alone, NOT whole-hub restore):
  - §6F RECOGNIZES / detects the disaster (events → incident).
  - Module disaster recovery then recovers by rolling back ONLY the affected
    product module’s data to a prior module snapshot.
  - Does NOT restore the whole hub database/code (§6B Restore).
  - Does NOT re-apply full release zips (§1C) — that is deploy rollback.

  Lifecycle:
    1) Detect — automated or manual security incident (§6F).
    2) Recognize disaster — attach affected_module + set disaster_recognized.
       High/critical auto-incidents may infer module from route/event and
       auto-recognize.
    3) Snapshot — module-scoped JSON of that module’s tables (+ checklist flag).
       Operators can snapshot anytime; recognition creates a baseline if none
       exists.
    4) Roll back — restore ONLY that module’s tables from a chosen snapshot.
       A pre-rollback safety snapshot of current module state is taken first.

  Recoverable modules (v1):
    module_general_compliance
    module_social_media_compliance
    module_website_compliance
    module_website_template_library
    module_social_media_template_library

  Capability:
    secmon_manage_disaster_recovery (Security Monitoring group; blurred while
    Functionalities → security_monitoring is OFF). Defaults ON for hub-admin
    style roles like other secmon_* manage caps.

  APIs (hub_can; also under /power-admin/* when acting):
    GET  …/security-monitoring/disaster-recovery/modules
    GET  …/security-monitoring/disaster-recovery/snapshots?module=
    POST …/security-monitoring/disaster-recovery/snapshots
         { module_key, label?, security_incident_id? }
    POST …/security-monitoring/incidents/{id}/recognize-disaster
         { affected_module }
    POST …/security-monitoring/incidents/{id}/rollback
         { snapshot_id }
    Declare incident accepts optional affected_module + recognize_disaster.

  UI (Security Monitoring → Incidents):
    - Declare: optional affected module + “Recognize as module disaster”.
    - Incident detail: Disaster recovery panel — recognize, create snapshot,
      select snapshot, Roll back module.
    - Incident list shows affected module + Disaster badge when recognized.

  Storage:
    - Table module_disaster_snapshots + files under
      storage/app/module-disaster-snapshots/{hub_id}/{module}/…
    - Incident columns: affected_module, disaster_recognized,
      disaster_recognized_at, recovery_status, last_snapshot_id,
      last_rollback_snapshot_id, last_rollback_at.

  Explicit non-goals (v1):
    - Whole-hub restore (§6B)
    - Full codebase rollback (§1C)
    - Multi-region failover / PITR beyond module snapshots

Actors:
  A   = Role WITH secmon_manage_disaster_recovery (+ security_monitoring ON)
  V   = Role with secmon view/manage incidents but WITHOUT disaster-recovery cap
  N   = No secmon caps / security_monitoring OFF
  PA  = power_admin acting via /power-admin mirrors

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS]

APIs / surfaces:
  /api/client-admin/security-monitoring/… (+ /power-admin/… mirrors)
  FE: Security Monitoring → Incidents → Disaster recovery panel
  Service: ModuleDisasterRecoveryService (MODULE_TABLES map)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

              archive restore (§6B)

SECTION 6A — Activity logging and anomaly detection  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary (product-area overview; detail in §6A.1 / §6A.2):
  Status:
    - Activity logging + activity report: IMPLEMENTED (BE + FE)
    - Anomaly detection (Activity Logs submodule / §6A.2): IMPLEMENTED (BE + FE)
  Cross-refs: §6F Security Monitoring (related but NOT the same), NFR-AUDIT 9.2,
  NFR-SEC 9.1; also distinct from Compliance audit trail (§6C) and Support
  Tickets (§3.3e).

  ONE product area with TWO parts:
    1) Activity logging — record and report what users/APIs did (audit trail)
    2) Anomaly detection — flag unusual patterns derived from that trail
  Part 2 lives as a submodule under Activity Logs (same nav area / capability
  family), not a replacement for Security Monitoring (§6F).

  How this relates to Security Monitoring (§6F) — MUST hold:
  - Activity Logs (6A) ≠ Security Monitoring (6F).
  - Activity Logs = general audit of product usage and mutations (“who did
    what”).
  - Security Monitoring = dedicated security-threat / incident module (events,
    incidents, thresholds, “Monitor now”, secmon_* caps,
    `security_monitoring` Functionality flag). It already detects some security
    anomalies (e.g. repeated failed logins, rate-limit trips) into
    `security_events`.
  - Anomaly detection under 6A focuses on unusual *activity-log* behaviour
    (usage / access patterns) and MAY hand off or link to a Security Monitoring
    event/incident when security-relevant.
  - Do NOT rebuild 6F inside Activity Logs; do NOT hide 6F behind activity-log
    UI.

Actors:
  V   = Role WITH dashboard_view_activity_logs
  N   = Role without that cap
  S   = Role WITH secmon_* + security_monitoring ON (Security Monitoring user)
  PA  = power_admin acting remotely when matrix allows

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS]

Surfaces:
  FE: Activity Logs page (feed + report + Anomalies tab/sibling)
  FE: Security Monitoring module (separate nav / secmon_*)
  FE: Compliance audit trail (§6C) — separate
  APIs: /activity-logs*, /activity-logs/anomalies* (detail §6A.1/§6A.2)
  APIs: /security-monitoring/* (detail §6F)

Next: §6A.1 Activity logging & activity report (below)
================================================================================

================================================================================
SECTION 6A.1 — Activity logging & activity report  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  Status: IMPLEMENTED (backend + frontend).
  - Every meaningful user activity is recorded in an activity_logs audit trail
    (per hub), including:
      • Auth: register, email verified, login OTP sent/verified, terms accepted,
        login, logout, failed login, blocked login (suspended / discontinued /
        invite-only / email unverified)
      • Successful mutating API actions (POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE): content,
        plans, settings, advisor import/discontinue, billing, capabilities,
        hubs, purchases, subscriptions, etc.
      • Document Repository / Firm Head (§3.4): appoint/replace/clear head
        (sync network_admin / firm_admin), grant/revoke member document rights,
        document add / delete / archive (and view/download if audited) —
        action keys firm.head.* and firm.documents.*
  - Sensitive fields (passwords, secrets, card data) are NEVER stored; payloads
    are redacted / truncated.
  - Activity report (aggregates): totals, unique users/actions, counts by
    action, by user, and by day — filterable by user, action, date range, and
    text search.
  - WHO CAN SEE logs/report: Capabilities matrix
    `dashboard_view_activity_logs` (hub-admin dashboard group). Applicable:
    finproms_admin, client_admin, manager, power_admin (when enabled for that
    hub / when acting remotely). PA toggles per hub/role on Central.
  - APIs (hub_can:dashboard_view_activity_logs):
      GET /api/client-admin/activity-logs (+ /power-admin/…) — paginated feed
      GET /api/client-admin/activity-logs/report (+ /power-admin/…) — aggregates
  - Frontend: Activity logs page under hub-admin and Power Admin shells, shown
    only when the viewer’s role has the capability.

Implementation notes (for testers):
  - LogApiActivity middleware logs successful mutating API requests (status
    <400); skips activity-logs paths, auth/* (explicit AuthController logs),
    stripe/webhook.
  - Explicit AuthController / Firm* services write richer action keys.
  - Viewing feed/report also writes activity_logs.view / activity_logs.report.
  - Redaction: SENSITIVE_KEYS + keys containing password/secret/api_key →
    [redacted]; large payloads truncated (_truncated).
  - Filters: user_id (0 = guest/null), action, q, from, to, per_page (1–100).
  - Firm visibility scoping may limit which logs an actor sees.

Cross-ref: Overview §6A; anomalies §6A.2; Compliance audit §6C; Firm docs §3.4;
  NFR-AUDIT 9.2; GDPR scrub/retention §6E.

Actors:
  V   = Role WITH dashboard_view_activity_logs
  N   = Role without the cap
  U   = Ordinary member performing actions
  PA  = power_admin with cap on acting hub

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [SEC]

APIs / surfaces:
  GET /api/client-admin/activity-logs
  GET /api/client-admin/activity-logs/report
  (+ /power-admin/… mirrors)
  FE: /my-dashboard activity logs (feed + report)
  Middleware: LogApiActivity
  Service: ActivityLogService

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6A.1-A  Capability + UI gate
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-6A.1-001 | [BE] With dashboard_view_activity_logs → GET feed + report 200
Steps: As V; GET activity-logs and /report
Expected: 200

TC-6A.1-002 | [BE] Without cap → both APIs 403
Steps: As N
Expected: 403

TC-6A.1-003 | [FE] Activity logs nav/page only when viewer’s role has the cap
Steps: Cap off/on for FA/CA/manager
Expected: Hidden then visible

             remotely
Steps: Enable for PA column on hub; act remotely; GET feed
Expected: 200; data from acting hub DB

TC-6A.1-005 | [BE] Cap is per hub/role — Shared matrix ON does not grant WL hub

================================================================================
SECTION 6A.2 — Anomaly detection (Activity Logs submodule)  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  Status: IMPLEMENTED (BE + FE) — Activity Logs → Anomaly detection tab;
  APIs under /activity-logs/anomalies*; artisan `activity:detect-anomalies`
  every 15 minutes + near-real-time hooks after selected activity_log writes.

  Goal: Surface unusual activity-log patterns (when / what pattern / who /
  status). Logging alone ≠ anomaly detection (§6A).

  Detector set (v1 — activity-pattern):
    A) Auth/access: unusual login volume vs baseline; new/rare IP or UA login;
       failed/blocked login burst (complements §6F; may link later)
    B) Privilege/admin: spike in high-impact actions; many distinct admin
       actions by same actor in a short window
    C) Data/export volume: unusual download/export/report-view from one user
    D) API/mutation volume: user/IP far above own recent action-rate baseline
  Out of scope v1 (owned by §6F / NFR-SEC-*): dependency “Monitor now”, malware/
  upload FW, full incident lifecycle UI, network IDS/SIEM.

  Enablement & permissions:
    - Under Activity Logs nav parent.
    - Default gate: dashboard_view_activity_logs (view feed + anomalies).
    - Optional later: dashboard_manage_activity_anomalies (not required in v1 —
      viewers with cap may mark reviewed/dismissed).
    - Does NOT require security_monitoring to VIEW anomalies.
    - Escalate → 6F requires security_monitoring ON + relevant secmon_* caps.

  Data model: activity_anomalies (per hub) with detected_at, window_*,
  anomaly_type, severity (info|low|medium|high), title/description,
  status (open|reviewed|dismissed|escalated), actor_*, source_ip,
  related_activity_log_ids, properties (baseline/observed), optional
  security_event_id / security_incident_id. Same redaction as activity_logs.
  GDPR scrub + retention align with activity_logs.

  UI: Anomalies tab/section; newest-first; filters type/severity/status/date/
  user/IP; detail with related logs; reviewed/dismiss; Escalate when 6F on;
  empty state with last detection run + “No open anomalies”.

  APIs (hub_can:dashboard_view_activity_logs):
    GET    …/activity-logs/anomalies
    GET    …/activity-logs/anomalies/{id}
    PATCH  …/activity-logs/anomalies/{id}   { status: open|reviewed|dismissed }
    POST   …/activity-logs/anomalies/{id}/escalate
    POST   …/activity-logs/anomalies/detect-now
    GET    …/activity-logs/anomalies/options
    GET/PUT …/anomaly-settings — optional v1 (NOT implemented today)
    (+ /power-admin/… mirrors)
  Activity log actions: activity.anomaly_escalate, activity.anomaly_reviewed
  (and detect_now / settings_update if present).

  Implemented anomaly_type keys (code):
    auth.failed_login_burst, auth.new_ip_login, admin.action_burst,
    data.export_volume, api.action_rate

Cross-ref: §6A overview; §6A.1 logging; §6F escalate target; GDPR §6E;
  NFR-AUDIT / NFR-SEC.

Actors:
  V   = Role WITH dashboard_view_activity_logs
  N   = Without that cap
  S   = V + security_monitoring ON + secmon rights for escalate path
  PA  = power_admin acting with view cap

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS] [SEC]

Next: §6B-SESSIONS Active sessions / force logout / session timeout (below)
================================================================================

================================================================================
SECTION 6B-SESSIONS — Active sessions / force logout / session timeout
                      [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  NOTE: PROJECT_REQUIREMENTS labels this “6B.” which collides with Hub Backup
  & Restore (§6B above). Test IDs use TC-6B-S-* / section 6B-SESSIONS.

  - Capability: dashboard_manage_active_sessions (Hub operations group).
    Applicable: all matrix roles; defaults ON for hub-admin style roles, OFF
    for approver / advisor / admin-staff / user until PA enables them.
  - Users with this capability can:
      • See which users currently have active login sessions (Sanctum tokens)
        on the current hub (or acting remote hub’s own database).
      • Force-logout any listed user — deletes all of that user’s tokens so
        they must sign in again.
      • Set this hub’s idle session timeout (minutes). Controls SPA cookie
        idle lifetime (session.lifetime) and Sanctum PAT expiry on that hub
        deploy. Stored on hubs.session_lifetime_minutes (null = fall back to
        SESSION_LIFETIME / config default, normally 7 days). Allowed range:
        15 minutes – 30 days (15–43200). When set from Central for a wired
        content hub, value is pushed via WhiteLabelHubSyncService. Saving the
        timeout immediately revokes all Sanctum tokens and cookie sessions on
        that hub (including the actor when not acting remotely) so everyone
        must sign in again under the new policy.
  - Force-logout of the viewer’s own account ends their session immediately.
  - Mutations → activity_logs: auth.force_logout, auth.session_timeout_updated.
  - APIs (hub_can:dashboard_manage_active_sessions):
      GET  /api/client-admin/active-sessions (+ /power-admin/…)
      POST /api/client-admin/active-sessions/{userId}/force-logout
      GET  /api/client-admin/session-settings
      PUT  /api/client-admin/session-settings { session_lifetime_minutes }
  - Frontend: /my-dashboard/active-sessions (nav + Capabilities gate) includes
    session timeout control for capable roles.

Actors:
  A   = Role WITH dashboard_manage_active_sessions
  N   = Role without the cap
  M   = Member with active Sanctum token(s)

SECTION 6D — One-time invoices (hub-wide)  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  - Capability: dashboard_view_one_time_invoices (Hub operations group).
  - Lists all invoices with Types = One time for the current hub:
      • post purchases
      • bundle purchases
      • one-time module charges (module_billing)
  - Distinct from:
      • My invoices (general_show_invoices / personal receipts only)
      • Module invoices (dashboard_view_module_invoices — one-time + recurring
        modules)
      • Advisor invoices (dashboard_view_advisor_invoices)
  - APIs (hub_can:dashboard_view_one_time_invoices):
      GET /api/client-admin/one-time-invoices (+ /power-admin/…)
      GET /api/client-admin/one-time-invoices/{id} (+ /power-admin/…)
  - Frontend: /my-dashboard/one-time-invoices (+ detail).

Implementation notes:
  - Filter: invoices.types = one_time (TYPES_ONE_TIME).
  - Content hub (not acting remotely): all one-time rows on that hub DB.
  - Central / acting remotely: scopes to module_billing rows for target hub_id
    (post/bundle purchases live on each content hub’s own DB).
  - show() 404 if not one_time or invoice not belonging to hub scope.
  - Detail may include payment + billing_breakdown for module_billing type.

Note: §2.6 Invoices (PDF/line-item deep product) remains SKIPPED in master
  progress; this section covers the hub-wide one-time list/detail capability
  surface only.

Actors:
  V   = Role WITH dashboard_view_one_time_invoices
  N   = Without that cap
  M   = Member with general_show_invoices (My invoices only)
  PA  = power_admin on Central / acting remotely

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE]

APIs / surfaces:
  GET /api/client-admin/one-time-invoices
  GET /api/client-admin/one-time-invoices/{invoice}
  (+ /power-admin/…)
  FE: /my-dashboard/one-time-invoices (+ detail)
  Related (must remain distinct): My invoices, Module invoices, Advisor invoices

Next: §6E UK GDPR / Data Protection (below)
================================================================================

================================================================================
SECTION 6E — UK GDPR / Data Protection (all hub types)  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary (overview; build phases in §6E.1):
  Regulator / law: UK GDPR + Data Protection Act 2018 (ICO). Product + process
  requirements for FinProms hubs. Legal copy must be reviewed by the customer’s
  counsel before go-live; engineering ships the tooling.

  Existing foundations that ALREADY help (do not rebuild):
    - Auth: Sanctum SPA cookies, email verification, optional login OTP (2FA)
    - Access control: role × Capabilities matrix
    - Audit: activity_logs + compliance_audit_events (FCA-style workflow trail)
    - Secret encryption at rest (Stripe, hub DB, cPanel); SecurityHeaders
    - Generic Compliance sensitive files + text encrypted at rest (NFR-GC-ENC /
      §9.3)
    - Terms accept gate (§2.2c) — contractual, not a privacy notice
    - Admin hard-delete of users (PA / WL remote) — not erasure

  Personal-data inventory (controllers must keep accurate):
    users; sessions/tokens; activity_logs; compliance_audit_events;
    invoices/billing; wc_template_requests.contact_details; firm_documents +
    SMC/GC/WC/support attachments; advisor_import_batches; hub_backups
    (encrypted; residual plaintext risk until retention); support_tickets.

  Lawful bases (document in Privacy Policy; typical):
    Contract; Legitimate interests (document LIA where used); Legal obligation;
    Consent only where relied on (prefer not invent marketing consent yet).

  FCA audit vs erasure tension (MUST follow):
    Erasure must ANONYMISE the person (e.g. “Deleted user”, clear email/IP)
    while keeping compliance event rows and FK-safe placeholders. Do NOT wipe
    the whole compliance trail on right-to-erasure.

Cross-ref: Privacy §2.2d; Cookies §2.2e; Terms §2.2c; phases §6E.1; out of
  scope §6E.2; breach runbook §6E.3; Security Monitoring §6F; backups §6B;
  activity logs §6A.1; compliance audit §6C.

Actors:
  ENG = Engineering / QA verifying tooling
  OPS = Controller / counsel (legal copy + inventory accuracy)
  G   = GDPR admin (dashboard_manage_gdpr) — detail §6E.1

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [PROCESS] [SEC]

SECTION 6E.2 — Out of scope for v1 (explicit)  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  Explicit non-goals for GDPR v1 tooling. Verifying these means confirming
  they are NOT implemented (and that listed existing protections remain).

  Out of scope:
    1) Field-level encryption of user name/email
       (hashed passwords + secret encryption already exist; GC files +
       submission body/feedback ARE encrypted — NFR-GC-ENC / §9.3)
    2) Rewriting historical hub_backup SQL dumps per erasure request
    3) Full RoPA UI inside the product (keep RoPA as an ops spreadsheet/doc)
    4) AI / analytics processors not currently integrated

Cross-ref: §6E inventory; §6E.1 Phase 3 backup residual risk; §6B backups;
  NFR-GC-ENC §9.3; Privacy processors §2.2d / §6E.1 Phase 1.

Actors:
  ENG = Engineering / QA (scope guardrails)
  OPS = Controller (RoPA spreadsheet ownership)

Layer tags: [PROCESS] [BE] [SEC]

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6E.2-A  Field-level encryption of name/email — NOT in v1
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-6E.2-001 | [PROCESS/BE] User name and email are stored as normal fields
              (not field-level encrypted) in v1
Steps: Inspect users schema / DB values after create
Expected: Plain (or app-level) name/email; not ciphertext-at-rest for those
          columns

TC-6E.2-002 | [SEC/PROCESS] Password hashing + secret encryption + GC
              file/body encryption remain the existing protections — not
              replaced by name/email FLE
Steps: Confirm password hashed; Stripe/hub secrets encrypted; GC attachments
       encrypted (NFR-GC-ENC)
Expected: Those protections still present; absence of name/email FLE is
          intentional scope, not a missing password/secret control

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6E.2-B  No rewrite of historical hub_backups on erasure
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-6E.2-003 | [BE] Erasing a user does not rewrite or scrub historical
              hub_backup archive files
Steps: Create backup containing user PII; erase user; compare backup file
       checksum/mtime/contents
Expected: Backup unchanged (residual risk documented in §6E.1 / §6B)

TC-6E.2-004 | [PROCESS] No API/job exists to “GDPR-scrub backups” in v1
Steps: Search codebase / admin UI for backup rewrite-on-erasure
Expected: Absent

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6E.2-C  No full RoPA UI in product
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TC-6E.2-005 | [FE] Product has no full Record of Processing Activities (RoPA)
              management UI
Steps: Search nav / GDPR page for RoPA editor
Expected: Not present; GDPR page is DSAR/erase/retention/cookies — not RoPA

TC-6E.2-006 | [PROCESS] RoPA kept as ops spreadsheet/doc owned by controller
Steps: Ops checklist
Expected: External RoPA exists or is explicitly tracked as legal/ops work
          (not blocked on engineering building in-app RoPA)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6E.2-D  No AI / analytics processors not currently integrated
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

              processors that are not integrated in the product
Steps: Compare Privacy defaults processor list vs actual integrations
Expected: Only real processors (Stripe, email, host/S3, cPanel, Central backup,
          etc.); no placeholder “AI vendor” unless actually used

              inventory + Privacy update (out of v1 scope until integrated)

Expected: Documented as future trigger; not silently claimed in v1

Next: §6E.3 Breach runbook (below)
================================================================================

================================================================================
SECTION 6E.3 — Breach runbook (ops checklist — not a product feature)  [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  Ops checklist for personal-data breach response. NOT a product feature to
  build. Verification = runbook documented, owners known, and product tools
  used correctly (6F / Support Tickets / GDPR page) without a second GDPR-only
  queue.

  Steps:
    1) Detect & contain (revoke access, rotate secrets, isolate). Prefer
       Security Monitoring (§6F) for detection evidence when enabled.
    2) Assess personal-data risk (what data, how many people, harm likelihood).
    3) If notification required, report to ICO without undue delay and within
       72 hours of becoming aware:
       https://ico.org.uk/for-organisations/report-a-breach/
    4) If high risk to individuals, inform them without undue delay.
    5) Record technical security event/incident in Security Monitoring (§6F)
       when functionality is ON. Track legal/ops GDPR follow-up in Support
       Tickets (or ops tracker) — do NOT invent a second GDPR-only queue.
    6) Handle related SARs / erasure via GDPR / data requests.
    7) Review lessons learned; confirm processor DPAs remain current.

Cross-ref: §6E.1 Phase 6; §6F Security Monitoring; Support Tickets §3.3e;
  GDPR tooling §6E.1 Phases 2–3.

Actors:
  OPS = Incident / DPO / counsel lead
  SEC = Security / platform eng using §6F
  G   = dashboard_manage_gdpr (SAR/erasure)
  ST  = Support Tickets capable staff (legal/ops follow-up)

Layer tags: [PROCESS] [FE] [BE]

SECTION 6F — Security Monitoring module (regular monitoring + incident reports)
             [DONE]
================================================================================
Requirement summary:
  Status: IMPLEMENTED (BE + FE). Cross-refs: §3.3f overview, §5.1 flag, §5.2
  caps, NFR-SEC §9.1, NFR-SEC-FW §9.5, module DR §6B.1, anomalies §6A.2,
  breach runbook §6E.3.

  PRIMARY SUITE: SECTION 3.3f (TC-3.3f-001 … TC-3.3f-048) already covers the
  combined 3.3f+6F requirement set (enablement, caps, events, incidents,
  report, alerts, access control gates, module DR gates, retention, E2E).
  Re-run / reuse those IDs as the core Pass/Fail pack for §6F.

  This §6F section adds gap / depth cases from the full §6F narrative that are
  only summarised in 3.3f, plus explicit cross-links.

  Purpose: regular detailed security monitoring per hub — events/incidents/
  dependency scan status. Separate from Activity Logs+anomalies (6A),
  Compliance audit (6C), Support Tickets. Escalate 6A.2 → 6F when
  security-relevant and security_monitoring ON.

  Enablement: Functionalities security_monitoring (default OFF); NOT a billable
  Modules package; Central/Shared/WL; while off secmon_* blurred / nav hidden /
  APIs 403.

  Caps: secmon_view_events, secmon_view_report, secmon_manage_incidents,
  secmon_manage_alerts, secmon_manage_disaster_recovery, secmon_manage_access.

  Event catalogue A–E; required event/incident fields; never store secrets.
  UI /my-dashboard/security-monitoring — Events / Incidents / Report; Monitor
  now; healthy empty state.
  Continuous detectors; scan-now / security:monitor for dependency summary;
  optional high+ alerts.
  Retention/GDPR anonymise; not ICO substitute (§6E.3).
  Out of scope v1: full SIEM/IDS/WAF replacement, packet capture, auto-patch
  without review, public status page.

Actors: same as §3.3f (VE/VR/MI/MA/MD/AC/X)

Layer tags: [BE] [FE] [FE+BE] [PROCESS] [SEC]
